RUN1 and RUN2 used a LangGraph-based multi-agent workflow combining Gemini 2.5 Flash and RigoChat-7B-v2, parallel generation strategies, internal quality signals, Event-Condition-Action routing, controlled editing and traceable decisions. RUN1 used the base workflow, while RUN2 activated an additional lexical-support layer based on a glossary and lexical resources. RUN3 was a RigoChat-based generate-evaluate-regenerate baseline with prompt engineering and LoRA-based adaptation. The official leaderboard reports BLEU-Orig, BLEU-Gold, SARI and BERTScore. During development, additional internal signals were also inspected, including semantic fidelity, readability, lexical simplicity, syntactic clarity and factual consistency. According to official SARI, RUN1 was the best HULAT2 run, with 44.0543 points, followed by RUN2 with 43.1049 and RUN3 with 38.5136. These results indicate that, in this task setting, signal-guided multi-agent routing outperformed the linear regeneration baseline. They also show that adding lexical support did not automatically improve reference-based scores. Further segment-level and document-level analysis are required to assess readability, factual consistency and user-oriented adequacy.

</details>


### [175] [Know Your Source: A Public Knowledge Store for Media Background Checks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02383)
*Benjamin Nichols,Michael Schlichtkrull,Nedjma Ousidhoum*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MEDIAREF，一个公开可用的网络文档知识库，用于支持对200个媒体来源进行可复现、低成本的媒体背景核查（MBC）生成评估，解决了现有基于LLM的检索增强生成事实核查中依赖昂贵专有搜索API的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于RAG的自动事实核查系统默认检索到的证据可靠，但现实中信息可能冲突、过时或来自不可靠/有偏见的来源。虽然已有工作提出通过媒体背景核查（MBC）评估信源可信度，但其生成依赖昂贵的专有搜索API，限制了可复现性。

Method: 构建MEDIAREF——一个覆盖200个媒体来源的公开网络文档知识库，设计可复现的构建与更新方法论，并在此知识库上对主流LLM进行MBC生成任务的自动评估与定性评估。

Result: MEDIAREF支持更高质量的MBC生成，通过自动和定性评估验证了其有效性，同时实现了低成本、可复现的评估流程。

Conclusion: MEDIAREF为MBC生成任务提供了可复现且低成本的评估基准，减少了对专有搜索API的依赖，有望推动源批判性推理在自动事实核查领域的发展。

Abstract: LLM-based retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is increasingly used for automated fact-checking (AFC) and related tasks. By grounding LLM outputs in retrieved evidence, RAG-based systems provide transparent justifications while allowing external information to be updated independently of the underlying model. However, existing approaches often assume retrieved evidence is reliable, although real-world information may be conflicting, outdated, and can originate from unreliable or biased sources. Recent work on *source-critical reasoning* addresses this challenge through media background checks (MBCs) (Schlichtkrull, 2024), which assess the credibility of evidence sources to support downstream fact verification. However, generating MBCs relies on costly proprietary search APIs, limiting reproducibility. To mitigate this issue, we introduce MEDIAREF, a publicly available knowledge store of web-sourced documents that enables reproducible, low-cost evaluation of MBC generation across 200 media sources. We describe a reproducible methodology for constructing and updating the collection, assess widely used LLMs on the MBC generation task, and demonstrate that MEDIAREF supports higher-quality MBC generation through both automatic and qualitative evaluation.

</details>


### [176] [The Future of NLP may not be at NLP Conferences: Scholarly Migration Patterns in Natural Language Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02416)
*David Jurgens*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究了LLM兴起后NLP研究的发表场所迁移现象，发现*ACL主会论文份额下降，而Findings子刊和通用ML会议份额显著上升，且ML会议存在引用溢价是驱动因素之一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的进展模糊了NLP与通用机器学习之间的学科边界，作者想探究NLP研究的"学科重心"是否正在从传统NLP会议向通用ML会议转移。

Method: 基于2010至2026年的NLP研究发表数据，从两个维度进行分析：(1) 比较LLM时代前后，资深作者在*ACL主会、Findings子刊、通用ML会议上的发表份额变化；(2) 追踪新锐作者（首作至少3篇NLP论文者）的发表场所分布变化（2019-2024）。同时使用因果推断方法估计通用ML会议对论文的引用溢价效应。

Result: 资深作者在*ACL主会的份额下降19.2个百分点，Findings子刊上升14.8个百分点，通用ML会议上升8.6个百分点。新锐作者中主要在*ACL发表的比例从84%（2019）降至74%（2024），而主要在通用ML会议发表的比例从5%升至21%。通用ML会议存在显著的引用溢价，影响作者的投稿选择。

Conclusion: NLP研究的发表场所正经历显著迁移：从传统*ACL主会向Findings子刊和通用ML会议转移，后者提供的引用溢价是重要驱动因素。

Abstract: Natural Language Processing (NLP) has traditionally been published in its core disciplinary venues like ACL. However, advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) has led to a blurring of the disciplinary lines between NLP and general Machine Learning (ML), with authors regularly publishing in venues from both fields. Here, we ask whether the disciplinary center of gravity is shifting. Using NLP research published from 2010 to 2026 and studies of both established and new authors, we find that a migration is taking place. First, comparing the pre- and post-LLM eras, established authors lost 19.2pp of share at flagship *ACL main-conference tracks while gaining 14.8pp in the newer Findings tracks, and general ML venues rose 8.6pp, even when adjusting for parallel growth in the fields. Second, among newer authors who debut with at least three first-author NLP-topic papers, the share whose work appears mostly at *ACL venues fell from 84% (2019) to 74% (2024), while the share appearing mostly at general ML venues rose from 5% to 21%. Using causal inference techniques, we estimate that these general ML venues confer a significant citation premium, which influences venue selection. Together, these results point to a significant shift in where NLP research is published.

</details>


### [177] [Language Models as Measurement Apparatus for Culture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02459)
*Kent K. Chang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文从Karen Barad的"能动切割"概念出发，论证NLP文化测量并非被动记录，而是通过模型、数据、标注、评估等装置主动参与构成文化现实。通过三个电视电影对话案例和三个装置自反性审视，提出一个理论驱动、经验严谨、文化具身的测量研究纲领。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语言模型被广泛用于量化文化现象，但学界对"什么使得这种测量具有文化独特性"缺乏深入反思。本文旨在揭示NLP文化测量的物质-话语实践本质，批判朴素实证主义立场，建立更具自觉性的方法论框架。

Method: 以Karen Barad的"能动切割"（agential cut）为核心理论框架，将NLP文化测量装置（模型、数据、标注、评估）分析为一种物质-话语实践。通过六个案例分析展开论证：三个案例聚焦电视与电影对话的文化测量（结构测量、互动测量、偏差测量），三个案例审视装置自身（文化标记的抹除、对历史材料的调谐、智能体工作流中的能动性）。

Result: 分析表明：(1)装置的实质性设计选择划定了现象与工具之间的边界；(2)该边界从一开始就是纠缠态的，因为语言模型已将大量被测文化材料内化；(3)每次"能动切割"同时是方法论承诺和伦理承诺。案例揭示了文化标记被系统性抹除、模型与历史材料之间存在特定调谐关系、以及智能体工作流中涌现的能动性等问题。

Conclusion: 提出一个理论驱动、实证严谨、文化具身的研究纲领，要求研究者将每次"能动切割"视为一种自觉承诺，同时承担方法论与伦理责任。NLP文化测量不应追求虚幻的客观性，而应在承认装置参与构成现实的前提下，建立更有反思性的测量实践。

Abstract: Language models are increasingly used to quantify cultural phenomena, but what makes such measurement distinctively cultural? This paper argues that NLP work on culture is a material-discursive practice: the apparatus -- model, data, annotation, evaluation -- participates in constituting the cultural reality it measures, rather than passively recording it. Drawing on Karen Barad's concept of the agential cut -- the contingent boundary between phenomenon and instrument -- I show that the apparatus's substantive design choices draw such boundaries, and that the boundary is entangled from the start because language models have already internalized much of the cultural material they measure. I illustrate this through three case studies on television and film dialogue (measuring structure, interaction, and deviation) and three examinations of the apparatus itself (erasure of cultural markers, attunement to historical material, and agency in an agentic workflow). This big picture analysis proposes a research program that is theory-driven, empirically rigorous, and culturally contingent, treating each agential cut as a conscious commitment, at once methodological and ethical.

</details>


### [178] [Will Scaling Improve Social Simulation with LLMs?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02464)
*Caleb Ziems,William Held,Su Doga Karaca,David Grusky,Tatsunori Hashimoto,Diyi Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过缩放定律研究LLM计算规模与社会模拟保真度之间的关系，发现多数行为和观点模拟任务随规模显著改善，但纵向预测和代表性不足的观点扩展较慢；行为模拟中的认知偏差校准和启发式学习方面，规模提升失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM社会模拟虽有前景但保真度不足，亟需厘清模型规模扩展能否弥合这些差距，还是社会模拟保真度与通用能力正交、需要独立的研究关注。

Method: 采用缩放定律方法，在三个子领域（观点建模、行为模拟、纵向预测）中，使用85个Qwen3架构的Transformer LLM（在DCLM语料上预训练，固定计算预算10^18至10^20 FLOPs）及35个更大开源模型（最高70B参数），从损失值预测下游准确率。

Result: 发现三个场景均存在显著的计算缩放效应；多数行为和观点模拟任务随规模快速提升，尤其涉及英文网络语料中代表性充分的人群时；纵向预测和代表性不足的观点扩展较慢；行为模拟中，风险厌恶等认知偏差校准和关联奖励启发式学习方面，即使微调模型从0.5B到8B参数也无明显提升。

Conclusion: 规模扩展将在多数场景改善社会模拟，但存在异常值，且在低资源领域改善的可靠性较低，这些方向值得独立研究关注。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) social simulations are a promising research method, but they are not yet faithful enough to be adopted widely. In this work, we investigate whether the current scaling paradigm in language modeling is likely to close these gaps, or whether simulation fidelity is orthogonal to general capabilities and therefore deserving of more research attention. We use scaling laws to study the relationship between LLMs' compute scale, general capability benchmarks, and the fidelity of social simulation in three representative sub-domains: opinion modeling, behavioral simulation, and longitudinal forecasting. Surprisingly, we discover strong compute scaling in all three settings, using a suite of 85 transformer LLMs with the Qwen3 architecture pre-trained on the DCLM web text corpus under fixed-compute budgets from $10^{18}$ to $10^{20}$ FLOPs. Then we evaluate 35 larger and more capable open-weight models up to 70B parameters, allowing us to predict downstream accuracy from loss. This reveals that the majority of behavioral and opinion simulation tasks will rapidly improve with scale, particularly when they involve populations that are well-represented in English web corpora. Longitudinal forecasting and underrepresented opinions scale more slowly, especially when they are less correlated with general knowledge and reasoning benchmarks like MMLU. In behavior simulation, scaling fails to improve model calibration with human cognitive biases like risk aversion, as well as human heuristics like learning correlated rewards from related tasks. On these tasks, even fine-tuned models fail to noticeably scale up performance from 0.5B to 8B parameters. Taken together, we conclude that scale will improve social simulations in most settings, but outliers exist, and improvements will be less reliable in low-resource domains.

</details>


### [179] [Audio-Based Understanding of Audiobook Narration Appeal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02473)
*Shahar Elisha,Mariano Beguerisse-Díaz,Emmanouil Benetos*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地通过计算手段研究有声书 narration 的声学特征（语调、语速、响度等）与消费数据之间的关系，发现声学信息对用户吸引力具有稳健的预测能力，且该效应在控制标题和体裁后依然显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 叙述（narration）是有声书聆听体验的核心，但叙述品质如何影响有声书的吸引力尚未被系统性地计算研究。不同体裁、不同书目、不同受众群体中，叙述特征的效果可能存在差异，需要数据驱动的方法来揭示这些规律。

Method: 从 LibriVox 平台提取有声书的声学和语音特征（如语调、语速、响度等），使用预训练音频模型进行特征抽取；将这些特征与消费数据（主要是 view-rate）进行关联分析，并考察体裁（genre）和标题（title）的交互效应；进一步使用更细粒度的专有参与度指标进行验证。

Result: 尽管消费数据有限，声学信息本身与吸引力之间存在稳健的关联，即便控制标题效应后该关联依然成立；使用专有细粒度参与度指标的验证进一步确认了上述发现。

Conclusion: 这是首个将叙述品质、体裁、标题与有声书消费行为联系起来的系统计算研究，证明了数据驱动方法在优化有声书个性化推荐和 narrator 选角方面的潜力。

Abstract: Narration is central to the audiobook listening experience, shaping how listeners engage with and understand the content. This work explores how narration qualities shape an audiobook's appeal, noting that their effects can vary by genre, title, and audience. We extract vocal and acoustic features (e.g., tone, pace, loudness) from LibriVox using pre-trained audio models and analyse their relationship with consumption data (specifically, view-rate) and their interplay with genre and title. Despite limited consumption data, we find that acoustic information alone has a robust association with appeal, even after accounting for title effects. We further validate these findings using more nuanced proprietary engagement metrics. To our knowledge, this is the first systematic computational study linking narration qualities, genre, title, and audiobook consumption, highlighting the potential of data-driven insights to improve audiobook personalisation and narrator casting.

</details>


### [180] [Visually Grounded Self-Reflection for Vision-Language Models via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02490)
*Liyan Tang,Fangcong Yin,Greg Durrett*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出VRRL强化学习框架，通过随机掩码轨迹前缀和经历回放缓冲区，增强LVLM在视觉推理中的自我反思能力，显著提升分布外场景下的视觉定位精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模视觉语言模型(LVLM)在思维链推理中虽能进行自我反思，但在反思阶段往往未能充分关注视觉输入，导致无法将反馈转化为基于视觉的修正，尤其在分布外图像上表现不佳。

Method: 提出VRRL（视觉反思强化学习）框架，包含两个核心组件：(1)训练时随机掩码思维链轨迹前缀，迫使模型从错误的中间预测中恢复；(2)引入基于经历回放缓冲区的缓冲roll-in机制，使模型暴露于多样化的失败状态以学习纠正。

Result: 在表格/图表视觉定位任务和空间导航基准上，现有模型和传统微调模型在分布偏移下性能大幅下降，而VRRL通过有效利用自我反思，在分布外准确率上显著优于标准RL和面向反思的微调基线。

Conclusion: VRRL通过显式训练视觉 grounded 的自我反思能力，有效解决了LVLM在分布外场景下的推理鲁棒性问题，为多模态推理的自我修正机制提供了新的训练范式。

Abstract: Large vision-language models can reason over multimodal inputs by generating textual chains of thought (CoT). A key capability exhibited in CoT reasoning is self-reflection: revisiting earlier decisions and correcting previous errors. However, existing LVLMs often fail to properly attend to visual inputs during reflection, limiting their ability to translate feedback into grounded corrections, especially for out-of-distribution images. To address this issue, we propose a novel reinforcement learning training framework VRRL, with two components explicitly designed to elicit visually grounded self-reflection. First, we randomly mask trajectory prefixes during training to emphasize recovery from incorrect intermediate predictions rather than making early mistakes. Second, we introduce buffered roll-ins from an experience replay buffer to expose the model to diverse failure states that it must learn to correct. We evaluate our approach on visual grounding tasks involving tables and charts, as well as spatial navigation benchmarks. While off-the-shelf and conventionally fine-tuned models degrade substantially under distribution shift, our method substantially improves average out-of-distribution accuracy over standard RL and reflection-oriented fine-tuning baselines by using self-reflection effectively.

</details>


### [181] [Reasoning LLM Improves Speaker Recognition in Long-form TV Dramas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02504)
*Yuxuan Li,Lingxi Xie,Xinyue Huo,Jihao Qiu,Jiacheng Shao,Pengfei Chen,Jiannan Ge,Kaiwen Duan,Qi Tian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了大规模说话人识别基准数据集DramaSR-532K（含53.2万条标注对话、900+角色），以及基于大型推理模型的DramaSR-LRM方法，通过多模态工具调用自主聚合上下文证据，在短对话等声学特征不可靠场景下显著超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长篇电视剧的复杂剧情理解高度依赖说话人识别——即将每句对话准确归因到对应角色。该任务需要融合听觉、语言和视觉多模态线索，但现有方法在短对话等声学生物特征不可靠的场景下表现不佳，亟需更大规模基准和更强推理能力的方法。

Method: (1) 构建DramaSR-532K基准数据集，包含532K条标注对话行，覆盖900+不同角色，强制要求融合听觉、语言、视觉三种模态线索进行说话人识别。(2) 提出DramaSR-LRM方法，基于大型推理模型（LRM），通过多模态工具调用机制自主聚合上下文证据，综合多种输入实现高保真说话人归因。

Result: 实验表明DramaSR-LRM显著优于现有基线方法，尤其在短对话场景（声学生物特征固有不可靠）中表现出色。

Conclusion: DramaSR-532K为多模态说话人识别提供了大规模评测平台，而DramaSR-LRM展示了大型推理模型通过工具调用整合多模态上下文进行鲁棒说话人归因的有效性。数据和代码将开源。

Abstract: Long-form TV dramas present a formidable challenge for comprehensive video understanding, where deciphering complex storyline often relies on \textbf{speaker recognition}, the task of accurately attributing each spoken utterance to its respective character. In this paper, we advance this field through two primary contributions. (1) We introduce \textbf{DramaSR-532K}, a large-scale benchmark comprising 532K annotated dialogue lines across more than 900 unique characters, necessitating the integration of auditory, linguistic, and visual cues for speaker recognition. (2) We propose \textbf{DramaSR-LRM}, a robust approach built upon a large reasoning model (LRM). DramaSR-LRM is designed to autonomously aggregate contextual evidence via multimodal tool-use, synthesizing diverse inputs to achieve high-fidelity attribution. Experimental results demonstrate that DramaSR-LRM significantly outperforms existing baselines, particularly on short utterances where acoustic biometrics are inherently unreliable. \textit{All the data and code will be made publicly available at the project page: https://www.github.com/198808xc/DramaSR-LRM.}

</details>


### [182] [LACUNA: A Testbed for Evaluating Localization Precision for LLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02513)
*Matteo Boglioni,Thibault Rousset,Siva Reddy,Marius Mosbach,Verna Dankers*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了LACUNA，首个具备参数级定位真值的遗忘测试基准。通过在OLMo模型中注入合成PII，发现现有SOTA遗忘方法虽输出表现好但定位不精确、易受重放攻击，而精准定位下简单梯度法也能实现稳健擦除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遗忘评估仅关注输出层面，无法判断知识是否真正从参数中擦除还是仅被混淆；重放攻击的成功加剧了这一担忧，亟需参数级定位真值来验证遗忘的彻底性。

Method: LACUNA通过掩码持续预训练，将合成个体的PII注入到1B和7B OLMo模型的预定义参数中，从而建立参数级定位真值。以此基准测试现有SOTA遗忘方法，并验证精准定位下简单梯度遗忘方法的有效性。

Result: 现有SOTA遗忘方法虽输出性能强劲，但参数定位高度不精确且易受重放攻击；而当定位准确时，简单的梯度遗忘方法即可实现强擦除和对重放攻击的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 精准的参数定位是遗忘成功的关键。LACUNA填补了行为评估的空白，为推进基于定位的鲁棒遗忘研究提供了重要基准。

Abstract: LLMs memorize sensitive training data, including personally identifiable information (PII), creating a pressing need for reliable post hoc removal methods. Unlearning has emerged as a promising solution, with state-of-the-art(SOTA) methods often following a localize-first, unlearn-second paradigm that targets specific model parameters. However, existing benchmarks evaluate unlearning solely at the output level, leaving open the question of whether unlearning truly erases knowledge from a model's parameters or merely obfuscates it, a concern reinforced by the success of resurfacing attacks. To bridge this gap, we introduce LACUNA: the first unlearning testbed with ground-truth parameter-level localization. LACUNA injects PII of synthetic individuals into predefined parameters of 1B and 7B OLMo-based models via masked continual pretraining, enabling direct evaluation of whether unlearning targets the weights responsible for knowledge storage. We use LACUNA to benchmark current SOTA unlearning methods and find that, despite strong output-level performance, existing methods are highly imprecise and susceptible to resurfacing attacks. We further show that when localization is successful, even a simple gradient-based unlearning method achieves strong erasure and robustness to resurfacing attacks, highlighting the importance of precise unlearning. We release LACUNA to complement behavioral evaluations and drive further advances in robust, localization-based unlearning.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [183] [Multilayer Q-Matrix-Embedded Neural Network for Cognitive Diagnosis (M-QCDNet): Structure-Aware Deep Learning Architecture for Psychometric Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01278)
*Yiyao Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出多层Q矩阵嵌入神经网络(M-QCDNet)，将Q矩阵作为结构先验融入深度学习，结合L2惩罚损失函数和可解释对齐评估指标，实现兼具心理测量透明性与神经网络灵活性的认知诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统认知诊断模型虽具有结构可解释性但灵活性受限，而深度神经网络虽预测能力强却缺乏心理测量学的理论支撑与可解释性。需要在两者之间建立桥梁，使AI认知诊断既透明又可解释、同时保持预测性能。

Method: 提出M-QCDNet，以Q矩阵作为结构先验嵌入多层神经网络，约束技能-题目关系的表征；设计带有L2惩罚项的损失函数，对与Q矩阵不一致的技能激活进行惩罚，以平衡预测精度与结构对齐；开发基于可解释对齐的评估矩阵，量化预测技能激活与题目层面技能的一致性程度。

Result: M-QCDNet在保持与认知理论一致的可解释潜在掌握模式的同时，实现了良好的预测性能；可解释对齐评估指标能有效量化模型诊断结果与Q矩阵结构的一致性。

Conclusion: M-QCDNet成功弥合了心理测量透明性与神经网络灵活性之间的鸿沟，为课堂实践提供了可操作的诊断工具，支持学习困难的早期发现与掌握性干预，推动了可解释、公平且可执行的AI认知诊断发展。

Abstract: The research proposes a multilayer Q-matrix-embedded neural network for cognitive diagnosis (M-QCDNet), which integrates the structural interpretability of cognitive diagnostic models (CDMs) with the deep learning neural network (NN). M-QCDNet structures the item-skill relationship using the Q-matrix as a structural prior, ensuring latent mastery profiles remain interpretable and consistent with cognitive theory, followed by the proposed loss function with an L2 penalty to penalize skills not aligned with the Q-matrix and to balance predictive performance and structural alignment. Corresponding evaluation matrices, the interpretable alignment-based metrics that quantify the degree to which predicted skill activations correspond to item-level skills, were further developed. M-QCDNet offers practical benefits for classroom practice, enabling early detection of learning difficulties and supporting mastery-based interventions. By embedding diagnostic validity into model design, M-QCDNet bridges psychometric transparency and neural flexibility, advancing interpretable, fair, and actionable AI for cognitive diagnostics.

</details>


### [184] [I\textsuperscript{2}RiMA: Spectral Riemannian Representation with Temporal Attention for Mental Stress Detection based on EEG Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01279)
*Cheng He,Kunyu Peng,Shangen Han,Jinming Ma,Jinhong Ding,Likun Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 I²RMA-Net，一种频点级空间协方差建模结合频率聚类聚合与切片注意力的黎曼流形网络，在跨被试 EEG 压力检测任务上以极低参数量达到 82.78% 的平衡准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨被试 EEG 压力检测面临两大挑战：(1) 压力相关模式既依赖被试又依赖频率，传统黎曼方法仅在时域建模空间协方差，忽略了关键的神经振荡信息；(2) 标准时序 token 化会破坏切片间的时间连贯性。需要一种能同时保留频率特异性与时间上下文的方法。

Method: 提出 I²RMA-Net，包含三个核心组件：(1) 在每个频率点独立构建空间协方差矩阵，映射到 SPD 切空间，保留通道几何结构与频率特异性判别线索；(2) 频率聚类聚合模块，自适应形成与 EEG 节律对齐的数据驱动频率簇，选择信息量大的频谱成分并减少冗余；(3) 片内-片间注意力模块，自适应融合局部切片级频谱动态与全局时间上下文。

Result: 在三个公开数据集上验证，I²RMA-Net 一致优于五种 SOTA 基线方法，最高达到 82.78% 的平衡准确率，且仅需 1.60M 参数和 31.95M FLOPs。

Conclusion: I²RMA-Net 通过频点级黎曼几何建模、频率聚类聚合以及片内-片间注意力机制，有效解决了跨被试 EEG 压力检测中频率特异性和时间连贯性两大难题，在性能和效率之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Cross-subject EEG stress detection remains challenging because discriminative stress-related patterns are both subject-dependent and frequency-specific. Conventional Riemannian methods model spatial covariance mainly in the time domain, overlooking neural oscillations that are critical for high-level cognitive state decoding, while standard temporal tokenization often fragments inter-slice temporal coherence. To address these limitations, we propose \method{}, an Intra-Inter Riemannian Manifold Attention Network for EEG-based stress detection. \method{} constructs spatial covariance matrices independently at each frequency point and maps them to the SPD tangent space, preserving channel-wise geometry together with frequency-specific discriminative cues. It further introduces frequency cluster aggregation to select informative spectral components and reduce redundancy by forming compact, data-driven frequency clusters aligned with EEG rhythms. Finally, an intra-inter slice attention module adaptively integrates local slice-level spectral dynamics and global temporal context across EEG sequences. Experiments on three datasets show that \method{} consistently outperforms five state-of-the-art baselines, achieving up to 82.78\% balanced accuracy while remaining efficient with only 1.60M parameters and 31.95M FLOPs.

</details>


### [185] [Fixed-Set Robustness in Programming by Example: Example Corruption and Semantic Partition Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01280)
*Yuan Si,Jialu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究PBE（示例编程）系统在对抗性示例损坏下的鲁棒性，形式化了有限版本空间上的最坏情况损坏，提出启发式损坏搜索与基于语义分区投票的防御（VPA），结果表明低边际PBE任务存在对抗脆弱性，VPA仅在保持分区投票边际时才有效，通常在实际任务中失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PBE鲁棒性研究通常将错误示例建模为随机噪声，但忽视了恶意对手有针对性选择损坏示例以最大化损害的场景。本文旨在揭示这一被忽视的对抗性鲁棒性维度。

Method: 形式化固定集合最坏情况损坏；在字符串变换DSL上实现精确有界池搜索和启发式损坏搜索；提出版本空间分区聚合（VPA）防御——在不相交示例组上合成并通过语义签名投票；在策划/生成的DSL任务、SyGuS PBE_SLIA切片、SYNTRA Playgol v2及噪声PBE基线等上进行实验评估。

Result: 一次策划编辑可翻转全部8个尖峰任务，而随机对照仅成功10.3%-16.7%；生成的低边际行在预算为1时即被翻转，但VPA可恢复；在公开SyGuS上投票边际接近1，自适应攻击使VPA准确率降为零；Playgol在141个接受行上显示正向配对bootstrap差距。小型精确输出提示实验在20个边际-1任务上，本地和API模型均呈现相同的干净到攻击退化模式。

Conclusion: 低边际PBE任务存在对抗鲁棒性维度，该维度被随机噪声评估所遗漏。语义分区聚合仅在干净语义保持分区投票边际时有效，在现实任务中常失败。整体结论审慎且有边界，部分否定。

Abstract: Programming-by-example systems infer programs from a small set of input-output examples. Robust PBE work usually models wrong examples as samples from a stochastic noise process and then minimizes an expected or empirical loss. This paper studies a different failure mode: an adversary who sees the synthesizer and chooses the examples whose corruption most damages the returned program. We formalize fixed-set worst-case corruption for finite PBE version spaces, implement exact-within-bounded-pool and heuristic corruption searches for a string-transformation DSL, and introduce version-space partition aggregation (VPA), a defense that synthesizes on disjoint example groups and votes by semantic signatures. The central claim is deliberately bounded and partly negative: low-margin PBE tasks have an adversarial robustness dimension that random-typo and noisy-PBE evaluations miss, while semantic partition aggregation helps only when the clean semantics keep a partition vote margin, which often fails on realistic tasks. Evidence from curated/generated DSL tasks, accepted public SyGuS PBE_SLIA slices, SYNTRA Playgol v2, and noisy-PBE objective baselines supports that boundary. One curated edit flips all 8 spike tasks while 200-trial typo, DSL-pool, and distance-matched random controls succeed on 10.3%, 11.0%, and 16.7%; generated margin-1 rows flip under budget 1 yet VPA recovers them; on public SyGuS the vote margin is near one, so an adaptive attacker drives VPA accuracy to zero; accepted public SyGuS slices move across exact-within-pool budget boundaries; and Playgol shows positive paired-bootstrap gaps against typo and same-pool random controls on the 141 accepted rows. A small exact-output prompt harness over 20 controlled margin-1 tasks shows the same qualitative clean-to-attacked pattern across local and API models, while it is treated as a scope check, not a broad LLM benchmark.

</details>


### [186] [Domain Knowledge Based Temporal-Spatial Graph Convolution Network for ECG Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01282)
*Wenting Ma,Zhipeng Zhang,Xiaohang Yuan,Ningwei Xie,Yuxin Xie,Xiaolin Wang,Meng Guo,Xingang Chai,Zhenjie Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于领域知识的图卷积网络用于心电图识别，通过PRQST关键点构建双流有向图（空间图建模关键点位置关系，时间图建模周期间时序依赖），在中文心电图智能竞赛数据集上取得了88.1%的总体F1分数，尤其在罕见类别上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI模型（尤其是端到端卷积神经网络）在医疗等专业领域中的可解释性不足，心电图识别需要融入领域知识来提升模型的可解释性和检测性能，特别是对罕见类别的识别能力。

Method: 提出基于领域知识的图卷积网络：1）引入PRQST关键标志点作为领域知识；2）构建双流有向图——空间有向图捕获关键点间的位置关系，时间有向图刻画相邻周期间的时序依赖；3）融合心电周期内（intra-cycle）和周期间（inter-cycle）两类信息进行分类。

Result: 在首届中国心电图智能竞赛数据集（9分类）上，总体平均F1分数达88.1%，罕见类别平均F1分数达76.3%，均优于现有最优模型，且领域知识的引入显著提升了罕见类别的检测性能。

Conclusion: 融入领域知识（PRQST关键点）的双流图卷积网络在心电图识别任务中优于传统端到端CNN方法，尤其在提升罕见类别检测性能和模型可解释性方面效果显著。

Abstract: In light of strides in Arti cial Intelligence (AI) and its wide spread application, challenges persist in the interpretability of AI models, particularly within specialized domains like healthcare, such as electro cardiograph (ECG) recognition. Rather than relying solely on end-to-end convolutional neural networks, this paper introduces a novel approach using a domain knowledge-based graph convolution network for ECG recognition. Key landmarks points of PRQST, vital to ECG interpreta tion, are incorporated as domain knowledge. The double-stream directed graph is employed to model both intra and inter ECG cycles. Speci cally, spatial directed graphs capture the positional relationships among key points, while temporal directed graphs delineate temporal dependencies between adjacent cycles in extended ECG sequences. Experimental re sults on the First Chinese ECG Intelligent Competition dataset, which speci cally classify ECG into nine categories, prove the e cacy of the proposed model. The overall average F1 score is 88.1%, the average F1 score of rare categories is 76.3%, both outperform the state-of-the-art models. The introduction of domain knowledge did enhance the detec tion performance, especially for rare categories.

</details>


### [187] [Scaling Laws for Grid-Based Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search in High Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01283)
*Matthew J Liu,Wei Hang Zheng,Vidhan Purohit,Siqi Xie,Chieh-En Li,Jerry Li,Noah Flynn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统地表征了多探针网格（multiprobe grid）算法在近似最近邻搜索中的扩展特性，揭示了在GloVe嵌入数据集上存在一个此前未被报道的维度扩展交叉点：多探针网格保持近似恒定的维度扩展指数，而其他基于图、树和分区的方法吞吐量则显著退化。该方法具有近线性的N扩展和较低的索引构建成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于网格的方法在现代ANN扩展性分析中长期缺位。现有方法（图、树、分区）在高维场景下吞吐量退化严重，且索引构建成本高。作者旨在填补这一空白，系统评估网格方法在N和d维度上的扩展行为，并探讨其对高效Transformer架构成本分析的潜在指导意义。

Method: 采用多探针网格算法（multiprobe grid），在GloVe嵌入数据集族上系统性地考察算法在数据集规模N和维度d两个维度上的扩展行为。通过与其他基于图、树和分区的方法进行吞吐量对比，揭示维度扩展交叉点（d-scaling crossover）现象。

Result: 实验发现了一个此前未被报道的维度扩展交叉点：多探针网格在维度增加时保持近似恒定的扩展指数，而其他方法吞吐量持续退化。查询扩展在N上接近线性，且索引构建成本低于竞争方法。

Conclusion: 多探针网格等基于网格的方法在频繁重建或高维场景中具有竞争力，尤其当索引成本和维度鲁棒性决定性能时。此外，鉴于自注意力机制已被形式化为ANN操作，ANN算法的N和d扩展特性可为高效Transformer架构的成本分析提供指导。

Abstract: Grid-based approaches to approximate nearest neighbor (ANN) search have been absent from modern scaling analyses. We present a systematic characterization of a multiprobe grid algorithm with respect to dataset size $N$ and dimensionality $d$. Our experiments reveal a previously unreported $d$-scaling crossover on the GloVe embedding family, in which multiprobe grid search maintains an approximately constant dimensional scaling exponent while other graph-, tree-, and partitioning-based methods exhibit degrading throughput. The advantage comes with near-linear query scaling in $N$, but also with lower indexing cost than competing ANN methods. Our results suggest that grid-based methods such as multiprobe grid may be competitive in rebuild-heavy or high-dimensional settings where indexing cost and dimensional robustness dictate performance. More broadly, recent work has formalized self-attention as an ANN operation. Thus, the $N$- and $d$-scaling properties of ANN algorithms may guide cost analysis of efficient transformer architectures. Code is available at: https://github.com/weiz345/MultiProbeANN.

</details>


### [188] [IonSense-QKG: A Quantum-Readiness Metadata Framework for Lithium-Ion Battery Dataset Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01286)
*Sakthi Prabhu Gunasekar,Prasanna Kumar Rangarajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 IonSense-QKG，一个面向锂离子电池数据集的量子就绪元数据框架，通过引入量子相关性元数据和量子就绪评分（QRS），帮助研究者从公共电池数据集中筛选适合混合量子-经典机器学习工作流的候选资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共锂离子电池数据集在化学体系、模态、规模、标签质量等方面差异巨大，直接影响其是否适用于混合量子-经典机器学习工作流。研究者缺乏系统性的方法来判断哪些电池数据集适合量子计算相关工作，需要一个量化的数据集筛选启发式框架。

Method: 基于 EV-Battery-IonSense 索引，构建量子就绪元数据框架 IonSense-QKG。为每条电池数据集记录补充量子相关元数据字段（任务类型、传感模态、化学体系、标签可用性、序列类型、预处理需求、候选量子编码、估计量子比特范围、NISQ 可行性）；引入透明的量子就绪评分（QRS）作为数据集选择的启发式指标；提供元数据表、评分脚本、稳健性检查、链接校验工具和 SQL 风格查询示例。

Result: 该框架实现了基于丰富元数据的查询式发现，可识别适用于紧凑量子特征映射、量子时间序列工作流、有限标签异常检测以及未来电池健康基准测试的数据集。

Conclusion: IonSense-QKG 将数据集选择定位为一个数据管理问题，为以数据为中心的量子电池分析提供了可复现的基础。

Abstract: Public lithium-ion battery datasets are increasingly used for state-of-health estimation, remaining-useful-life prediction, anomaly detection, electrochemical diagnostics, second-life analytics, and battery safety research. However, these datasets vary substantially in chemistry, modality, scale, label quality, sequence structure, access status, and preprocessing complexity. These differences directly affect whether a dataset is feasible for near-term hybrid quantum-classical machine-learning workflows.
  This paper presents IonSense-QKG, a quantum-readiness metadata framework for lithium-ion battery dataset discovery. Starting from the EV-Battery-IonSense index, the proposed framework enriches public battery dataset records with quantum-relevant metadata, including task type, sensing modality, chemistry, label availability, sequence type, preprocessing requirements, candidate quantum encodings, estimated qubit range, and NISQ feasibility. A transparent Quantum Readiness Score is introduced to rank datasets as candidate resources for future hybrid quantum-classical battery benchmarks. The score is intended as a dataset-selection heuristic, not as evidence of quantum advantage.
  The framework demonstrates query-based discovery over enriched metadata to identify datasets suitable for compact quantum feature maps, quantum time-series workflows, limited-label anomaly detection, and future battery-health benchmarking. The released artifact includes metadata tables, scoring scripts, robustness checks, link-checking utilities, and SQL-style query examples. IonSense-QKG positions dataset selection as a data-management problem and provides a reproducible foundation for data-centric quantum battery analytics.

</details>


### [189] [A Novel Machine Learning Approach for Central Nervous System Tumor Classification from DNA Methylation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01307)
*Paulo R. Ferreira,Lucas Coutinho Freitas,Laís dos Santos Gonçalves,William Borges Domingues,Lucas Petitemberte de Souza,Mariana B. Michalowski,Vinicius F. Campos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于稀疏随机投影降维与多项逻辑回归相结合的甲基化脑肿瘤分类方法，在91类分类任务上类别级准确率达86%、家族级准确率达93%，分别比现有最优方法提升约4和5个百分点，具有临床显著性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于DNA甲基化谱的中枢神经系统肿瘤分类方法在跨队列可迁移性、方法论严谨性及多类别鲁棒评估方面仍存在不足，需要一种方法论更扎实的机器学习方法来提升分类性能。

Method: 提出将稀疏随机投影（Sparse Random Projection）用于高维甲基化数据的降维，然后结合多项逻辑回归（Multinomial Logistic Regression）进行91类CNS肿瘤分类。在2,801例参考队列上采用分层3折交叉验证，并在1,104例独立临床评估队列上进行外部验证。

Result: 在参考队列上达到96%的平均准确率；在独立临床评估队列上，91类级别准确率为86%，甲基化类别家族级别准确率为93%，相比现有最优方法（分别为82%和88%）分别提升约4和5个百分点。

Conclusion: 该方法在方法论上更加严谨，在所有评估设置中一致优于现有最优方法，5个百分点的提升在临床诊断中可直接影响肿瘤亚型判定及后续治疗决策，能够实质性提升CNS肿瘤分类的可靠性。

Abstract: NA methylation profiling has become a powerful approach for central nervous system (CNS) tumor classification, yet important challenges remain regarding cross-cohort transferability, methodological correctness, and robust multiclass evaluation. In this work, we propose a novel and methodologically rigorous machine-learning approach for methylation-based CNS tumor classification that combines Sparse Random Projection for dimensionality reduction with multinomial logistic regression for classification. We evaluate the proposed approach in the same general experimental setting established by a widely used reference classifier. On the 2,801-sample reference cohort, our method achieves a mean accuracy of 96\% under stratified 3-fold cross-validation. On the independent 1,104-sample clinical evaluation cohort, it reaches 86\% accuracy at the 91-class level and 93\% when predictions are evaluated at the methylation class family level. These results improve upon the corresponding state-of-the-art reference figures of 82\% class-level concordance and 88\% family-level concordance, yielding absolute gains of approximately 4 and 5 percentage points, respectively. This improvement is clinically relevant: in a diagnostic setting, a 5-point increase in correct tumor classification can directly affect cancer subtype assignment and, in turn, influence treatment selection and downstream clinical decision-making. Our results show that the proposed model, grounded in stronger methodological practice in machine learning, consistently outperforms the previous state of the art across evaluation settings and can materially improve the reliability of CNS tumor classification.

</details>


### [190] [From Approximation to Emergence: A Theory of Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01311)
*Zhilin Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一部关于深度学习理论的专著摘要，系统性地梳理了从经典近似理论、优化和泛化基础，到过参数化、鲁棒性、生成模型、Transformer、上下文学习、缩放定律、可解释性、对齐及涌现等当代议题的统一数学框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习已超出任何单一数学解释的范畴，现有文献结果孤立分散，缺乏一个统一的、以证明为导向的理论叙事。本书旨在将广泛的文献组织为连贯的研究脉络，为研究者提供一张严谨的深度学习理论地图。

Method: 本书采用统一的理论框架，逐一审视每个理论所控制的对象、使其有效的假设条件以及其未能解释的现象。通过这一分析范式，将近似理论、优化、泛化、过参数化、鲁棒性、生成建模、Transformer、上下文学习、缩放定律、可解释性、对齐和涌现等主题串联为连贯的研究叙事。

Result: 本书呈现了深度学习理论的当前图景：强大但不完整，且日益聚焦于一个核心问题——学习到的机制如何从规模、数据、架构和训练中涌现出来。

Conclusion: 深度学习理论正处于从碎片化解释走向统一叙事的过渡阶段，其中心问题是理解规模驱动的涌现机制。该专著为研究者提供了系统性的理论路线图。

Abstract: Deep learning has outgrown any single mathematical explanation. From Approximation to Emergence develops a unified, proof-oriented account of modern deep learning theory, tracing a path from the classical foundations of approximation, optimization, and generalization to the contemporary mechanisms of overparameterization, robustness, generative modeling, transformers, in-context learning, scaling laws, interpretability, alignment, and emergence. Rather than presenting isolated results, the book organizes a broad literature into a coherent research narrative: each theory is examined through the object it controls, the assumptions that make it valid, and the phenomena it leaves unexplained. Written for researchers, graduate students, and mathematically trained practitioners, this monograph offers a rigorous map of deep learning theory as it stands today: powerful, incomplete, and increasingly centered on the question of how learned mechanisms arise from scale, data, architecture, and training.

</details>


### [191] [Conditional Inference Trees and Forests for Feature Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01417)
*Robert Milletich,Justin Downes,Steve Goley,Newel Hirst*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了条件推断树(CIT)和条件推断森林(CIF)作为top-k特征排序方法的性能，证明Bonferroni校正的+1蒙特卡洛置换p值可在完全置换零假设下控制节点拒绝率。在多个基准测试中，CIF在分类任务中排名第4（17种方法/22个数据集），在回归任务中排名第3（18种方法/8个数据集）。消融实验表明，自适应停止和使用精确阈值搜索对运行时间影响最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CIT和CIF通过置换检验选择分裂特征来减少分裂偏差，但重复的置换检验和阈值搜索导致计算开销较大。现有研究对其作为top-k特征排序方法在下游预测任务中的系统评估不足，且各计算组件对运行时间和预测性能的贡献尚不清楚。

Method: 作者采用三类实验设计：(1)在22个分类数据集和8个回归数据集上，将CIF与17种分类方法和18种回归方法进行下游预测性能对比；(2)通过消融实验量化自适应停止、阈值搜索数量等组件对运行时间和预测分数的影响；(3)通过稀疏高维模拟实验检验森林特征采样是否会导致信息性特征被遗漏在分裂决策之外。理论层面，证明了在固定节点的特征和置换预算不依赖节点响应的条件下，Bonferroni校正的+1蒙特卡洛置换p值可控制节点拒绝率。

Result: CIF在下游预测基准中表现优异，分类排名第4、回归排名第3。消融实验显示：关闭自适应停止使拟合时间增加4.0-8.4倍，使用精确阈值搜索增加1.9-10.8倍，而下游分数变化最多仅0.011。高维模拟表明，森林特征采样可能使信息性特征在大量分裂决策中被遗漏。

Conclusion: 结果支持CIF作为top-k特征排序方法在下游预测基准中的有效性。Bonferroni校正可在完全置换零假设下保证统计控制，而自适应停止和阈值搜索策略是影响计算效率的关键因素，且对预测性能影响极小。

Abstract: Conditional inference trees (CIT) and conditional inference forests (CIF) reduce split-selection bias by testing features before choosing split thresholds, but repeated permutation tests and threshold searches can make these methods computationally expensive. We study CIT and CIF as top-$k$ feature-ranking methods for downstream prediction using real-data benchmarks, runtime ablations, and synthetic feature-recovery experiments. At a fixed node, if the features and permutation budget do not depend on the node responses, Bonferroni-corrected $+1$ Monte Carlo permutation $p$-values control nodewise rejection under the complete permutation null. CIF ranks 4th among 17 classification methods on 22 datasets and 3rd among 18 regression methods on 8 datasets. With Bonferroni correction held fixed, the CIF runtime ablations indicate that adaptive stopping and the number of thresholds searched have the largest measured effect on runtime: turning off adaptive stopping and using exact threshold search increase fitting time by 4.0--8.4$\times$ and 1.9--10.8$\times$, respectively, while downstream score changes are at most 0.011. Sparse high-$p$ simulations indicate that forest feature sampling can leave informative features out of many split decisions. Overall, the results support CIF as a top-$k$ feature-ranking method in the evaluated downstream prediction benchmarks.

</details>


### [192] [Black-Box Inference of LLM Architectural Properties with Restrictive API Access](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01313)
*Christopher Ellis,Shreyas Chaudhari,Mei-Yu Wang,Leighton Barnes,Giulia Fanti,José M. F. Moura*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NightVision 提出了一种在黑盒受限 API（仅暴露单个 token 的 logit，无 logit bias 功能）下恢复 LLM 架构参数（隐藏维度、深度、参数量）的攻击方法，利用"公共集合提示"技术和谱分析推断隐藏维度，并结合 TTFT 测量估计深度和参数量，在 32 个开源模型上取得较低误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前商业 LLM 提供商为防御架构泄露，已将其 API 限制为仅暴露每个解码 token 的单个 logit，并移除 logit bias 功能。然而，作者质疑这种限制是否足以完全隐藏底层模型架构，旨在探索在现有最严格 API 条件下架构参数是否仍可被恢复。

Method: 提出 NightVision 攻击，包含三个核心步骤：(1) "公共集合提示"技术——构造多个不同提示，使它们在输出中共享同一组 token，从而获取这些公共 token 的 log 概率；(2) 对获取的 log 概率矩阵进行谱分析（奇异值分解），利用奇异值谱的突变点推断前馈网络的隐藏维度；(3) 结合端到端首个 token 延迟（TTFT）测量与已估计的隐藏维度，通过推理时间建模反推模型深度和总参数量。

Result: 在 32 个开源 LLM 上评估：隐藏维度估计的平均相对误差为 23%（MoE 模型仅 9%）；对于超过 30 亿参数的模型，深度和参数量的估计误差在 53% 以内。消融实验展示了估计精度随 token 预算和模型特性变化的规律。

Conclusion: 当前商业 LLM API 的访问限制仍不足以完全隐藏底层架构信息，攻击者可在最严格黑盒条件下恢复关键架构参数，提示 API 提供商需进一步加强防御措施。

Abstract: In practice, most commercial LLM providers do not publicly release details of underlying LLM architectures. However, prior work has shown that given limited API access to an LLM (namely, top-$k$ logits and/or a logit bias function), one can recover certain architectural details of an LLM, such as the hidden dimension of the feed-forward network. Perhaps in response to these results, most commercial LLM providers have restricted their APIs to expose only the single logit for each decoded token, and they no longer give users the ability to bias logits. We show that even under current restrictive APIs, several architectural parameters are still recoverable. We present NightVision, an attack that uses restrictive black-box API access to estimate the hidden dimension, depth, and parameter count of an LLM. Algorithmically, NightVision relies on a novel common set prompting technique in which multiple prompts expose log probabilities for the same set of output tokens; a spectral analysis of these results is used to infer hidden dimension. NightVision additionally uses end-to-end time to first token (TTFT) measurements and the estimated hidden dimension to estimate depth and parameter count. We empirically evaluate NightVision on 32 open-source LLMs, recovering hidden dimension to within 23% average relative error across all models (9% on MoE models), and depth and parameter count to within 53% for models exceeding three billion parameters. We run extensive ablations to demonstrate how these accuracies scale with token budget and model properties. Overall, our results suggest that current LLM APIs are not sufficiently restricted to fully obfuscate the architectural details of their underlying models.

</details>


### [193] [How to Allocate Your Tokens? Scaling Laws with Training Steps and Batch Size](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01487)
*Fabian Schaipp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种"三项缩放定律"，将模型大小、训练步数和批次大小三个因素同时纳入考量，能够准确恢复最优批次大小的缩放规律，且仅需较少的训练运行即可进行鲁棒拟合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的缩放定律通常只关注模型大小和训练数据量两个维度，忽略了批次大小这一关键超参数的影响。作者希望建立一个更全面的缩放定律，能够显式刻画批次大小与模型性能之间的关系，同时降低拟合定律所需的实验成本。

Method: 提出"三项缩放定律"（three-term law），将训练数据显式拆分为训练步数和批次大小两个独立维度。通过在大量训练运行上拟合该定律，验证其对最优批次大小缩放行为的恢复能力，并利用次优批次大小的训练数据实现更鲁棒的拟合。进一步从三项定律推导出次优批次大小下的缩放规律。

Result: 三项定律能够正确恢复最优批次大小的缩放行为；相比传统方法，可用显著更少的训练运行量进行鲁棒拟合；推导出的次优批次大小缩放规律与先前关于临界批次大小的实证发现一致。

Conclusion: 三项缩放定律通过显式引入批次大小维度，在保持拟合效率的同时，统一了最优与次优批次大小下的缩放行为描述，为理解大规模训练中的超参数选择提供了更完整的理论工具。

Abstract: We propose a scaling law that takes into account model size and training data while explicitly splitting the latter into training steps and batch size (called three-term law). Fitting the proposed law on a large set of training runs, we find that it correctly recovers the scaling of the optimal batch size. Moreover, because it makes use of training runs with suboptimal batch size, our proposed law can be robustly fit with a significantly smaller amount of training runs. We further show that the three-term law can be used to derive scaling laws for suboptimal batch sizes, and that it matches previous empirical findings related to the critical batch size.

</details>


### [194] [Multi-modal Rail Crossing Safety Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01365)
*Paimon Goulart,Chansong Lim,Nícolas Roque dos Santos,Yue Dong,Sheldon Peterson,Jia Chen,Evangelos E. Papalexakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个概念验证流水线，利用铁路道口的图像和结构化事故报告等多模态数据，通过微调的紧凑型视觉语言模型（VLM）来评估铁路道口的安全性。该系统在区分高低风险道口上达到宏F1分数0.757，在估计FRA安全评分上RMSE为0.078，相关系数0.492，且定性结果与领域专家评估一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁路道口安全评估传统上依赖人工检查或结构化数据，但视觉信息（道口图像）中蕴含丰富的安全相关线索尚未被充分利用。作者旨在探索如何结合视觉线索与结构化事故数据，构建能够自动评估道口安全性并与FRA标准及专家判断对齐的AI系统。

Method: 提出一条多模态数据驱动的概念验证流水线：首先进行数据准备（收集道口图像与FRA事故报告），然后采用路由微调（routed fine-tuning）策略训练紧凑型视觉语言模型（VLM），使其能够融合视觉特征和结构化数据，输出安全风险分类（HIGH-RISK/LOW-RISK）及FRA安全评分估计。

Result: 系统在高低风险分类任务上达到宏F1分数0.757；在FRA安全评分回归任务上RMSE为0.078、相关系数为0.492；定性分析显示模型输出与领域专家评估高度一致。

Conclusion: 该概念验证系统证明了多模态学习方法在铁路道口安全评估中的可行性，为构建可对齐FRA标准和专家判断的自动化AI评估工具奠定了基础，同时揭示了数据准备、学习范式等方面的关键研究挑战。

Abstract: Given one or more images of a railway crossing, can we leverage visual cues that allow us to robustly estimate how safe it is? Can we improve our ability to do so by introducing structured data (such as official accident reports) about the accident history of that crossing into our models? In this work, we explore how to best answer those questions towards building an AI system that can ingest multi-modal data for railway crossings and provide safety assessment and scores that align with expert opinion and with safety scoring used by the Federal Railroad Administration (FRA). To that end, we propose a proof-of-concept pipeline that delivers on that goal, while at the same time exploring and tackling a number of critical research challenges that pertain to different parts of the pipeline, from data preparation to different learning paradigms that can allow us to realize such a system. Indicatively, our proposed system identifies HIGH-RISK and LOW-RISK crossings with a macro F1 score of 0.757 and estimates FRA-based safety scores with an RMSE of 0.078 and correlation of 0.492 using a routed fine-tuned compact VLM pipeline, while producing qualitative results that align with domain-expert assessment.

</details>


### [195] [Unveiling the Non-Monotonic Effect of Privacy on Generalization under Byzantine Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01492)
*Thomas Boudou,Batiste Le Bars,Nirupam Gupta,Aurélien Bellet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了拜占庭鲁棒性、本地差分隐私与泛化误差之间的关系并非简单的三难困境，而是取决于隐私强度：强隐私（高噪声）下，增加隐私反而降低泛化误差；弱隐私（低噪声）下，隐私与鲁棒性才存在张力。该现象通过算法稳定性分析得到理论证明与实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作证明拜占庭鲁棒性、本地差分隐私(LDP)与优化误差之间存在三难困境，但该结论是否适用于泛化误差尚不明确。本文旨在填补这一理论空白，澄清隐私-鲁棒性-泛化三者之间的真实关系。

Method: 作者通过分析LDP约束下拜占庭鲁棒分布式学习的算法稳定性（algorithmic stability），推导出泛化误差的匹配上下界，以此揭示不同隐私强度下鲁棒性与隐私之间关系的相变行为。

Result: 理论证明：(1)高噪声（强隐私）机制下，增加隐私会降低泛化误差，鲁棒性与隐私之间无张力；(2)低噪声（弱隐私）机制下，隐私-鲁棒性之间的张力重现，增强隐私会损害泛化性能。该非单调行为获得了理论与实验的双重支持。

Conclusion: 拜占庭鲁棒性、LDP与泛化误差之间的三难困境并非普遍成立，而是高度依赖于隐私机制的强度。这一发现修正并细化了先前关于三者关系的理解，对设计隐私保护的鲁棒分布式学习系统具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: Recent work has established a fundamental trilemma between Byzantine robustness, local differential privacy (LDP), and optimization error in distributed learning. We show that this trilemma does not universally extend to generalization error, but instead depends critically on the privacy regime. Specifically, in the high-noise regime (strong privacy), we prove that increasing privacy reduces the generalization error, i.e., there is no tension between robustness and privacy. In the low-noise regime (weaker privacy), however, the tension between robustness and privacy reappears and increasing privacy indeed degrades generalization. Our theory explains this surprising non-monotonic behavior of the generalization error via matching lower and upper bounds on the algorithmic stability of Byzantine-robust distributed learning under LDP constraints. We corroborate and further analyze these theoretical findings with empirical evaluations.

</details>


### [196] [How Should Transformers Encode Numeric Values in Electronic Health Records?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01391)
*Maria Elkjær Montgomery,Christian Igel,Mikkel Odgaard,Martin Sillesen,Mads Nielsen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了Transformer模型中离散、连续和混合三种数值编码策略，在合成算术任务和真实临床预测任务上进行评估。研究发现，显式建模数值-概念交互的方法在精度敏感任务中表现最佳，而混合分词方法（保留数值但先分箱再投影）提供了更稳健、更普适的替代方案，最优分箱数遵循关于数据集规模的幂律分布。总体而言，实用中的稳健性和可部署性往往比极端数值精度更重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于Transformer的电子健康记录（EHR）序列建模中，如何有效编码数值（如实验室检验值）是一个尚未被系统研究的基础问题。现有方法在离散化、连续表示和混合策略之间缺乏统一比较，本文旨在填补这一空白，揭示不同编码策略在精度、优化稳定性和架构灵活性之间的权衡。

Method: 作者构建了嵌入真实EHR数据的合成算术任务，以及真实临床预测任务，系统比较三类编码策略：(1) 离散编码（如直接分箱/离散化）；(2) 连续编码（如直接使用数值嵌入）；(3) 混合编码（保留数值但先分箱再投影，即hybrid token-based方法）。重点评估了各策略在数值精度、训练稳定性和架构适应性上的表现，并实证推导了最优分箱数与数据集规模之间的幂律关系。

Result: （1）显式建模数值-概念交互的方法在精度敏感的算术任务上表现最佳，但受架构限制；（2）混合分词方法（先分箱再投影）提供了更稳健且广泛适用的替代方案；（3）最优分箱数遵循关于数据集规模的幂律分布；（4）模型普遍展现的是"足够好"的数值计算能力，而非精确算术；（5）引入实验室检验值的临床收益因任务而异。

Conclusion: 在EHR的Transformer建模实践中，追求极端数值精度并非最优策略，稳健性与可部署性更为关键。混合分词式数值编码方法（hybrid token-based approach）因其良好的鲁棒性和广泛适用性，可作为实际应用中的默认选择。

Abstract: How do we encode numeric values in transformer-based sequence processing, particularly in electronic health record (EHR) data? We systematically compare discrete, continuous, and hybrid value encoding strategies using synthetic arithmetic tasks embedded within real-world EHR data, as well as real-world clinical prediction tasks. Our study reveals trade-offs between numeric precision, optimisation stability, and architectural flexibility. We find that approaches that explicitly model value-concept interactions perform best on precision-sensitive arithmetic tasks when architectural constraints permit. Hybrid token-based approaches that retain numeric values but apply binning prior to projection provide a more robust and broadly applicable alternative, with the optimal number of bins following a simple empirically derived power-law in dataset size. Across tasks, models consistently exhibit reliable "good enough" numeric computation rather than exact arithmetic, while clinical gains from incorporating laboratory values are task-dependent. This suggests that robustness and deployability often outweigh maximal numeric precision in practice, motivating hybrid token-based approaches as a practical default.

</details>


### [197] [Role-Aware Neural Convex Divergence Heads for Asymmetric Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01762)
*He Huang,Lu Shen,Yunfeng Huang,Li Qi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种角色感知的神经凸散度头（role-aware neural convex divergence head），用于非对称表示学习。该头在计算输入凸神经Bregman散度之前，先进行源角色和目标角色投影，在角色投影空间中产生非负结构化得分，在词汇蕴含、句子蕴含、本体层次等有向关系任务上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准欧氏距离、余弦相似度和马氏距离等度量头都是对称的，无法建模有向关系；而通用神经打分器虽能建模方向性，却缺乏几何结构。已有方法（如双曲嵌入、有序嵌入）提供了一定方向性但存在各自局限。因此需要一种既能建模方向性、又具备丰富几何可解释结构的表示学习模块。

Method: 提出角色感知神经凸散度头：1）将输入表示分别通过源角色投影和目标角色投影映射到角色空间；2）在角色投影空间内计算输入凸神经网络（ICNN）参数化的Bregman散度；3）输出非负有向得分。论文进一步分析了该结构的理论性质，包括投影空间恒等性、源角色凸性、方向间隙分解以及基于Hessian的局部曲率特征。

Result: 在词汇、句子、本体和有向图四个基准上进行实验，覆盖十个随机种子。结果显示：角色感知投影相比普通ICNN-Bregman头在语义和本体基准上一致提升了方向准确率，同时保持零负散度率。但在大规模固定特征的引文预测任务上，专门的对称或双曲基线方法在排序准确率上仍占优，这被识别为一个边界情形。

Conclusion: 该角色感知神经凸散度头最适合被理解为一个结构化、可解释的即插即用距离模块，适用于方向性关系重要的任务场景。其在多数有向语义任务中表现稳健，但在某些特定大规模固定特征场景下并非最优，需根据任务特点选择。

Abstract: Many representation learning problems involve directed relations, such as lexical entailment, sentence entailment, ontology hierarchy, and citation links. Standard Euclidean, cosine, and Mahalanobis heads are symmetric, while generic neural scorers can model directionality but provide limited geometric structure. This paper proposes a role-aware neural convex divergence head for asymmetric representation learning. The head applies source- and target-role projections before evaluating an input-convex neural Bregman divergence, yielding a nonnegative structured score in the role-projected space. We characterize its projected-space identity, source-role convexity, directional-gap decomposition, and Hessian-based local curvature. Experiments on lexical, sentence, ontology, and directed graph benchmarks compare symmetric distances, unstructured asymmetric scorers, order/hyperbolic baselines, plain ICNN-Bregman heads, and the proposed role-aware variant. Across ten random seeds on the main semantic and ontology benchmarks, role-aware projections consistently improve directional accuracy over plain ICNN-Bregman heads while preserving zero observed negative divergence rate. The results also identify a boundary case: on large fixed-feature citation prediction, specialized symmetric or hyperbolic baselines remain stronger in ranking accuracy. Overall, the proposed head is best understood as a structured and interpretable plug-in distance module for tasks where directional relations matter.

</details>


### [198] [NeuroBridge: Bridging Multi-Task MRI Knowledge for Neurodegenerative Disease Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01401)
*Mengyu Li,Guoyao Shen,Chad W. Farris,Xin Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NeuroBridge 是一个临床引导的多任务 MRI 框架，通过整合大规模自监督预训练与海马体分割、萎缩分类及重建目标，在 AD/MCI 分类中取得领先性能，并展示了跨队列泛化和基于概率的机会性筛查可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于 MRI 准确识别阿尔茨海默病（AD）、轻度认知障碍（MCI）及相关痴呆仍面临挑战，因为疾病相关的脑结构变化常具有细微性和异质性，常规单任务方法难以捕捉。

Method: 提出 NeuroBridge 框架：(1) 大规模自监督 MRI 预训练；(2) 多任务学习，同时优化海马体分割、海马体萎缩分类和图像重建目标；(3) 门控融合（gated fusion）微调策略。在 ADNI 和 OASIS 两个队列上进行评估，包括跨队列迁移、基于预测概率的分析和机会性筛查实验。

Result: NeuroBridge 在所有分类任务中取得最高性能：ADNI 中 AD vs. 认知正常对照组准确率达 88.17%，OASIS 中达 82.78%。最大增益出现在 MCI 相关及混合诊断场景。框架展现出强跨队列泛化能力，预测类别概率与准确率之间存在系统性关联，验证了基于概率的机会性筛查可行性。

Conclusion: 临床引导的多任务表示学习优于传统单任务方法，NeuroBridge 为痴呆评估和基于 MRI 的机会性筛查提供了稳健且可扩展的框架。

Abstract: INTRODUCTION: Accurate MRI-based identification of Alzheimer's disease (AD), mild cognitive impairment (MCI), and related dementias remains challenging because disease-related structural changes are often subtle and heterogeneous. We developed NeuroBridge, a clinically guided multi-task MRI framework for neurodegenerative disease diagnosis. METHODS: NeuroBridge integrates large-scale self-supervised MRI pretraining with hippocampal segmentation, hippocampal atrophy classification, and reconstruction objectives, followed by gated fusion fine-tuning. Performance was evaluated across ADNI and OASIS cohorts, including cross-cohort transfer, probability-based analysis, and opportunistic screening. RESULTS: NeuroBridge achieved the highest performance across evaluated classification tasks, reaching 88.17% accuracy for AD versus cognitively normal controls in ADNI and 82.78% in OASIS. The largest gains occurred in MCI-related and mixed-diagnosis settings. The framework demonstrated strong cross-cohort generalization, systematic associations between predicted-class probability and accuracy, and the feasibility of probability-based opportunistic screening. DISCUSSION: Clinically guided multi-task representation learning improves neurodegenerative MRI diagnosis beyond conventional single-task approaches. NeuroBridge provides a robust and scalable framework for dementia assessment and MRI-based opportunistic screening.

</details>


### [199] [Regularized Variational and Spectral Log-Density-Ratio Estimation in the Gaussian Location Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01895)
*Francis Bach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在高斯位置模型中，本文比较了变分估计与谱估计两种脊正则化对数密度比估计方法。通过推导高维渐近等价公式，发现：观测数较多时，变分估计风险更小；观测数较少时，谱估计因其基于协方差的构造具有更低方差而更优。本文还探讨了核范数惩罚用于特征学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对数密度比估计在机器学习中有广泛应用，但在高维场景下，不同估计方法的相对优劣尚不清晰。作者旨在从理论上系统比较变分估计与谱估计在脊正则化框架下的风险表现，为实际应用中的方法选择提供指导。

Method: 在高斯位置模型 q~N(0,I)、p~N(Δ,I) 的仿射不变设定下，研究两种估计量：(1) 变分估计量——基于经验KL散度拟合加L2平方惩罚；(2) 谱估计量——将单一变分问题替换为一族脊正则化最小二乘问题。利用凸-高斯极小极大定理(CGMT)推导变分极限的标量熵最小化表征，利用加权样本协方差矩阵预解式的确定性等价推导谱极限。在观测数与维数同比例趋于无穷时获得高维渐近等价公式，并通过固定信号纵横比扫描与正则化优化进行风险比较。

Result: (1) 变分估计的渐近极限由标量熵最小化问题刻画；(2) 谱估计的渐近极限由两个独立高斯样本协方差矩阵加权和的预解式确定性等价给出；(3) 观测数多时变分估计风险更小，观测数少时谱估计更优；(4) 核范数惩罚可部分实现特征学习。

Conclusion: 在本文所分析的准则和渐近框架下，变分估计与谱估计各擅胜场：大样本下变分估计因模型设定正确而具有更低风险，小样本下谱估计凭借协方差构造带来的低方差优势而胜出。两种方法的优劣取决于样本量与维度的相对比例。

Abstract: We study ridge-regularized log-density-ratio estimation in the Gaussian location model with a common covariance matrix. By affine invariance, the model is written as q $\sim$ N(0, I), p $\sim$ N($Δ$, I), with linear features, where $Δ$ is a mean vector. The variational estimator is the empirical Kullback-Leibler (KL) log-normalized fit with a squared L2-penalty on its nonconstant coefficient, and the spectral estimator recently introduced in [1] replaces a single variational problem by a continuum of ridge-regularized least-squares problems. We derive high-dimensional deterministic asymptotic equivalents when the numbers of observations and dimension tend to infinity with fixed ratios. The regularized variational limit is characterized by a scalar entropy minimization problem derived from the convex-Gaussian-min-max theorem (CGMT), while the regularized spectral limit follows from deterministic equivalents for resolvents of weighted sums of two independent Gaussian sample covariance matrices. We use these formulas to compare population risks, with experiments focused on fixed-signal aspect-ratio sweeps and optimized regularization. Our conclusion is that with many observations, under the criteria and asymptotic regimes analyzed here, the well-specified variational estimator has the smaller risk, while with fewer observations, the spectral estimator is favored because its covariance-based construction has lower variance. We also study how a nuclear penalty can be used and partially analyzed to perform feature learning.

</details>


### [200] [Spin-Weighted Spherical Harmonics Enable Complete and Scalable $\mathrm{E}(3)$-Equivariant Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01408)
*Chenxing Liang,Yuchao Lin,Andrii Kryvenko,Wendi Yu,Chuan Li,Jianwen Xie,Xiaofeng Qian,Shuiwang Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SpinGTP 提出一种基于自旋加权球谐函数（SWSH）的广义Gaunt张量积方法，在保持GTP的渐进效率的同时，恢复了CGTP中被GTP丢失的反对称路径，实现了完整的E(3)等变性表达，特别适用于手性材料和非中心对称几何结构的模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有E(3)等变网络中，Clebsch-Gordan张量积（CGTP）因O(L⁶)计算复杂度难以扩展，而Gaunt张量积（GTP）虽降低了复杂度，却无法捕获反对称路径，导致表达力不完整，尤其影响对手性材料等任务的建模能力。

Method: 从标量球谐函数推广到自旋加权球谐函数（SWSH），利用SWSH的代数性质恢复反对称相互作用，同时保持GTP的渐进效率；构建更具表达力的等变基函数，自然包含张量积的奇宇称分量。

Result: 在Tetris、3BPA、SPICE-MACE-OFF和OC20等多样化基准上，SpinGTP达到与完整CGTP相当的精度；在涉及手性材料和非中心对称几何结构的任务中表现尤为优越。

Conclusion: SpinGTP为大规模3D原子系统模拟提供了一条完整、可扩展且数学上严谨的高阶等变性实现路径。

Abstract: $\mathrm{E}(3)$-equivariant networks are promising for 3D atomistic system modeling, yet their scalability is limited by the $O(L^6)$ complexity of the Clebsch-Gordan Tensor Product (CGTP). The recently proposed Gaunt Tensor Product (GTP) reduces the complexity but is unable to capture the antisymmetric paths, resulting in incomplete expressivity. In this work, we present SpinGTP, an approach to overcome the GTP incompleteness by generalizing from scalar functions to Spin-Weighted Spherical Harmonics (SWSH). By relying on the algebraic properties of SWSH, SpinGTP recovers the missing antisymmetric interactions while maintaining the asymptotic efficiency of GTP. It also allows for a more expressive equivariant basis that naturally accounts for the parity-odd components of tensor products. We evaluate SpinGTP across diverse benchmarks, including Tetris, 3BPA, SPICE-MACE-OFF, and OC20. Our results show that SpinGTP achieves accuracies comparable to full CGTP. Notably, by explicitly capturing antisymmetric paths, SpinGTP exhibits superior performance in tasks involving chiral materials and non-centrosymmetric geometries. This work provides a complete, scalable, and mathematically rigorous path toward high-order equivariance in large-scale 3D atomistic system simulations.

</details>


### [201] [The Rollout Infrastructure Tax in Coding-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01415)
*Daniel Thi Graviet,Lovre Pesut,Ivan Dagelic,Vedran Jukic,Ivan Burazin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文比较了四种代码执行基础设施（单容器、托管沙箱、Kubernetes编排容器和云虚拟机）在强化学习训练中的开销，发现冷启动延迟差异高达110倍，完成百万级轨迹的工时差异达1.8倍，提出应将执行基板作为训练系统的一部分进行优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码智能体强化学习方法将执行基础设施视为背景实现细节，忽视了其开销。由于RL后训练需要大量交互式软件rollout，每次rollout的微小开销在规模下会累积成显著的效率差异，因此有必要系统性地评估不同执行基板的实际成本。

Method: 对四种执行基板（单容器、托管沙箱、Kubernetes编排容器、云虚拟机）进行横向对比研究，测量冷启动延迟和完成百万级150步轨迹所需的投影工时。

Result: 冷启动延迟在不同基板间差异高达110倍；完成一百万条150步轨迹的投影工时差异达1.8倍。

Conclusion: 未来的编码智能体RL系统应将执行基板的优化纳入训练系统本身的设计考量，而非仅将其视为部署层面的辅助设施。

Abstract: Coding-agent reinforcement learning treats execution infrastructure as a background implementation detail, despite relying on large numbers of interactive software rollouts. This is a missed opportunity: measuring infrastructure overhead can reveal practical efficiency gains for RL post-training, where small per-rollout savings compound at scale. We present a comparative study of four execution substrates: single containers, hosted sandboxes, Kubernetes-orchestrated containers, and cloud virtual machines. We find up to $110\times$ variation in cold-start latency and a $1.8\times$ spread in projected worker-hours for one million 150-step trajectories. Our results suggest that future coding-agent RL systems should optimize execution substrates as part of the training system itself, not merely as deployment plumbing.

</details>


### [202] [On the Utility and Factual Reliability of Pruned Mixture-of-Experts Models in the Biomedical Domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01444)
*Atsuki Yamaguchi,Szymon Palucha,Léo Bijar,Aline Villavicencio,Nikolaos Aletras*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探究了结构化专家剪枝对 Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) 模型在生物医学等高风险领域中效用与事实可靠性的影响，发现适度剪枝可维持领域内效用，但极端剪枝会增加幻觉风险，且跨领域泛化时效用与可靠性均快速下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MoE 剪枝研究主要关注基准效用，忽视了剪枝对模型事实可靠性（幻觉风险）的影响，尤其在高风险的生物医学领域缺乏系统评估。作者旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在四种 MoE 模型上应用六种剪枝方法，设定多种剪枝比例，分别在生物医学（域内）和通用（跨域）场景下进行生成与分类任务评估，同时衡量效用和可靠性。

Result: 适度剪枝可在不立即损害可靠性的前提下保持域内效用；极端剪枝比例下幻觉风险显著增加；跨域迁移时，效用与可靠性均急剧退化。

Conclusion: 安全压缩高度依赖任务与领域，仅凭效用指标评估剪枝后的 MoE 模型不足以支撑高风险部署，必须引入可靠性评估。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models offer inference speedups via selective activation but impose substantial memory requirements because the whole network must remain loaded. Structured expert pruning is a practical approach for reducing deployment costs in resource-constrained settings. However, prior studies primarily evaluate benchmark utility, leaving the effect of pruning on factual reliability underexplored, particularly in high-stakes domains such as biomedicine. In this paper, we investigate how domain-specific expert pruning affects both utility and reliability. We assess four MoE models, six pruning methods, and multiple pruning ratios across generation and classification tasks under in-domain (biomedical) and cross-domain settings. Results reveal that moderate pruning preserves in-domain utility without immediate reliability decline, although hallucination risks increase at extreme pruning ratios. When shifting to the general domain, both utility and reliability degrade rapidly. These findings indicate that safe compression depends heavily on the task and domain. Evaluating pruned MoE models solely on utility is inadequate for high-stakes deployment without reliability assessment.

</details>


### [203] [Geometry-Aware R-Structured Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01449)
*Sergei Kucherenko,Nilay Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GRS-KAN架构，将R函数与KAN结合，通过可微的逻辑运算显式编码几何约束，在含间断的回归任务中相比标准KAN将测试RMSE降低高达67%，同时提升可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准KAN擅长学习平滑非线性结构，但难以显式表达间断、可行域和隐式几何边界等先验几何约束。R函数恰好能以解析方式编码这类几何/逻辑约束，二者结合可弥补KAN的这一不足。

Method: 提出GRS-KAN架构，将V.L. Rvachev的R函数（通过R-合取与R-析取实现可微逻辑运算）集成到KAN框架中。KAN分支学习平滑非线性，R函数分支解析编码几何/逻辑约束，两者以加性、乘性或不可知分支加权等方式融合，实现几何支撑的显式表示。

Result: 在圆形和矩形支撑的含间断回归问题上，GRS-KAN相比标准KAN测试RMSE降低高达67%，边界定位精度显著提升。不可知变体可自动判断几何先验是否有益于当前任务。

Conclusion: 显式几何编码能显著提升预测精度与边界定位能力，同时通过解析表示学到的几何结构增强可解释性，验证了将领域先验以R函数形式注入神经网络的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: We propose a novel hybrid neural architecture, the Geometry-aware R-Structured Kolmogorov-Arnold Network (GRS-KAN), which integrates V.L.Rvachev's R-functions into the Kolmogorov-Arnold Network (KAN) framework. The proposed approach combines two complementary modeling mechanisms: smooth nonlinear structure is learned by KAN branches, while known geometric or logical constraints are encoded analytically using differentiable R-functions. This enables explicit representation of discontinuities, feasible regions, and implicit geometric boundaries within a trainable neural architecture.
  The framework implements differentiable logical operations through R-conjunctions and R-disjunctions, allowing complex geometric supports to be represented analytically and incorporated directly into regression models. Several GRS-KAN variants are introduced, including additive, multiplicative, and agnostic branch-weighted architectures.
  The method is demonstrated on regression problems involving discontinuities with circular and rectangular supports. Numerical experiments show that explicit geometric encoding substantially improves predictive accuracy and boundary localization compared with standard KANs. In the considered benchmarks, geometry-aware GRS-KAN models reduce test RMSE by up to 67% while simultaneously improving interpretability through explicit analytical representation of the learned geometric structure. The agnostic variant further demonstrates the ability to automatically determine whether geometric priors are beneficial for a given learning task.

</details>


### [204] [Token Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01455)
*Kathan Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Ember——一种针对语言模型中嵌入表和LM-head矩阵的轻量级优化器。Ember利用嵌入层独特的梯度几何结构，仅需O(V+D)显存（远少于Adam的O(2VD)），无需分片token表优化器状态。研究表明token的优化轨迹可用简单的一维射线描述，挑战了神经网络参数在高度非凸空间中导航的普遍观点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在语言模型中，嵌入表和LM-head矩阵（接口层）的梯度几何结构与密集隐藏层权重存在本质差异，但现有优化器（如Adam）将它们一视同仁，导致大量显存浪费在嵌入层的优化器状态上（O(2VD)）。作者希望利用这一结构差异，设计专用优化器以在不牺牲性能的前提下大幅降低显存开销，并提升从监督微调、强化学习到预训练全流程的Pareto前沿。

Method: 提出Ember优化器，核心思路基于嵌入层梯度的低秩/低维特性：仅需O(V+D)显存维护优化器状态，而非Adam的O(2VD)。具体来说，Ember捕捉token优化轨迹的一维射线结构，用极少量参数（仅千字节级优化器状态）即可有效更新嵌入表和LM-head。该方法可与现有ZeRO/FSDP分布式训练框架无缝集成，无需对嵌入表优化器状态进行分片。

Result: 实验表明Ember在不同batch size和模型参数量下均能有效扩展，在监督微调、RL和预训练等多种场景中，性能与Adam相当或更优，同时仅使用千字节级的优化器状态。此外，实证分析揭示token的优化轨迹可被简单的一维射线很好地描述，说明Transformer训练的优化器设计空间比先前认知的要窄得多。

Conclusion: 嵌入层和LM-head的梯度几何结构与密集层根本不同，利用这一特性设计的Ember能以极小显存代价实现与Adam竞争的性能。研究表明Transformer训练中真正有效的优化器空间比想象中更窄，一维射线模型即可描述token优化轨迹。作者已开源分布式Ember实现，便于与现有训练框架集成。

Abstract: Language models learn continuous programs over discrete symbols, with the embedding table and LM-head acting as the read/write interface between them. We show that this interface has gradient geometry distinct from dense hidden weights which can be exploited to improve the Pareto frontier across supervised finetuning, RL, and pretraining, while only utilizing kilobytes of optimizer state. We introduce Ember, a lightweight optimizer for embedding and LM-head matrices that utilizes O(V + D) VRAM, instead of Adam's O(2VD), and forgoes the need to shard both token table optimizer states. We provide empirical evidence that Ember scales effectively across batch size and parameter count. We show that the optimization trajectory of tokens can be well described by a simple 1D ray, counter to the popular belief that neural net parameters navigate a heavily nonconvex landscape. We provide a principled view on the surprisingly narrow space of optimizers that suffice for Transformer training. Finally, we open-source our distributed Ember implementation that merges cleanly with existing ZeRO/FSDP setups to support further research at https://github.com/katop1234/ember

</details>


### [205] [Class-Grouped Normalized Momentum and Faster Hyperparameter Exploration to Tackle Class Imbalance in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01474)
*Haemin Park,Diego Klabjan,Martin W. Braun,Xiuqi Li,Balakrishnan Ananthanarayanan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedCGNM 是一种联邦学习客户端优化器，通过按类分组、组内动量归一化来均衡多数类和少数类的梯度幅度，并缓解稀有类梯度噪声；配合基于 XAB 的 FedHOO 算法优化重采样率，在长尾分布场景下显著提升分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中的类别不平衡问题因隐私和异构性约束无法使用集中式方法解决，导致少数类预测性能差；现有方法在多类别长尾场景下未能有效均衡梯度贡献并处理稀有类梯度的噪声。

Method: 提出 FedCGNM：将类别按最小化组内方差划分为少量组，每组维护一个动量并归一化到单位长度，以各组归一化动量的加和作为更新方向。同时给出考虑时变重采样率的理论收敛分析。进一步提出 FedHOO：基于 X-armed-bandit 的算法，利用联邦并行性以线性代价评估每客户端两个候选重采样率的组合，高效优化小规模联邦中的重采样率。

Result: 在四个公开长尾基准数据集和一个私有芯片缺陷数据集上，FedCGNM 一致优于基线方法；在小规模联邦中，FedHOO 带来进一步的性能增益。

Conclusion: FedCGNM 通过类分组动量归一化有效应对联邦长尾学习挑战，FedHOO 则进一步提升了小规模联邦场景下的优化效率，两者结合为联邦类别不平衡问题提供了一个实用且理论完备的解决方案。

Abstract: Class imbalance poses a critical challenge in federated learning (FL), where underrepresented classes suffer from poor predictive performance yet cannot be addressed by standard centralized techniques due to privacy and heterogeneity constraints. We propose FedCGNM (Federated Class-Grouped Normalized Momentum), a client-side optimizer in FL that partitions classes into a small number of groups based on minimum within-group variance, maintains a momentum per group, normalizes each group momentum to unit length, and uses the summation of the normalized group momentums as an update direction. This design both equalizes gradient magnitude across majority and minority groups and mitigates the noise inherent in rare-class gradients. We further provide a theoretical convergence analysis explicitly accounting for time-varying resampling-rates. Additionally, to efficiently optimize these rates in small-client regimes, we introduce FedHOO, an X-armed-bandit (XAB) based algorithm that exploits federated parallelism that evaluates many combinations of two candidate rates per client at linear cost. Empirical evaluation on four public long-tailed benchmarks and a proprietary chip-defect dataset demonstrates that FedCGNM consistently outperforms baselines, with FedHOO yielding further gains in small-scale federations.

</details>


### [206] [Don't Let Gains FADE: Breaking Down Policy Gradient Weights in RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01490)
*Juliette Decugis,Sean O'Brien,Francis Bach,Gabriel Synnaeve,Taco Cohen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出统一框架解构优势函数，揭示符号轴与难度轴上的双重权衡，并据此设计自适应优势函数 FADE，在推理基准上显著加速收敛并提升准确率-多样性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习后训练虽能提升 LLM 推理能力，但存在训练不稳定和多样性崩溃问题。优势函数方法种类繁多，缺乏系统性理解，研究者不清楚何时该用哪种优势函数。

Method: 提出统一框架，将任意优势函数沿符号轴（正/负梯度质量）和难度轴（难题聚焦程度）进行分解，揭示训练过程中探索阶段偏好平衡与难题聚焦、利用阶段偏好抑制与中等聚焦的动态权衡。基于此设计 FADE（Focal Advantage with Dynamic Entropy），一种能自动读取训练动态并调度梯度权重的自适应优势函数。

Result: FADE 在 7B 规模上比最佳静态基线提前 20k 步达到峰值 pass@1，在 32B 规模上提前 2k 步；同时在 LiveCodeBench 和 AIME 的所有 pass@k 指标上实现最佳准确率-多样性权衡。

Conclusion: 优势函数的设计可通过符号与难度两个正交轴进行系统化理解，训练过程中的动态权衡驱动了对自适应优势函数的需求。FADE 通过感知训练阶段自动调度梯度权重，在多个基准上验证了其优越性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning post-training dramatically improves LLM reasoning, but suffers from training instability and diversity collapse. Advantage functions offer an appealing fix: they reshape the training objective, reweight which rollouts drive learning, and are trivial to implement. Yet a proliferation of methods makes it unclear which advantage to use and when. We cut through the confusion with a unifying framework that decomposes any advantage into its positive and negative gradient mass along two orthogonal axes. On the sign axis, imbalanced updates collapse either entropy or weight geometry. On the difficulty axis, hard-problem focus sharpens signal but costs sample size. Both trade-offs shift during training: exploration favors balance and hard focus; exploitation favors suppression and medium focus. This motivates FADE (Focal Advantage with Dynamic Entropy), a self-adapting advantage that reads training dynamics to schedule the gradient weight automatically. FADE reaches peak pass@1 20k steps earlier than the best static baseline at the 7B scale and 2k steps earlier at the 32B , while achieving the best accuracy-diversity trade-off across all pass@k on LiveCodeBench and AIME.

</details>


### [207] [Towards Learning Representations of Policies in Two-Player Zero-Sum Imperfect-Information Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01498)
*Kevin Wang,Kevin Yang,Arjun Prakash,Amy Greenwald*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究双人零和不完美信息博弈中策略表征（嵌入）的学习问题，提出策略数据集的构建方法、策略表征的学习方法以及评估表征质量的下游任务，并在Kuhn和Leduc扑克上进行实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在博弈论和AI研究中，如何有效地表示和学习策略（行为策略的嵌入）是一个重要但未被充分探索的问题。有用的策略表征可以捕捉策略之间的行为相似性和差异性，为策略分析、策略迁移等任务提供基础。本文旨在系统性地探索自监督学习方法在博弈策略表征学习中的应用，填补该领域的空白。

Method: 论文提出三方面方法：(1) 策略数据集构建：针对给定博弈生成多样化的策略集合，形成可用于训练的数据集；(2) 策略表征学习：采用自监督学习方法从策略数据中学习嵌入表示；(3) 下游任务评估：设计一系列下游任务来验证所学表征的有效性。实验在Kuhn和Leduc Poker两个标准测试平台上进行。

Result: 尽管方法较为基础，实验结果表明学到的嵌入中确实存在有意义的行为表征，能够反映出策略之间的内在结构和行为特征。这验证了自监督学习用于博弈策略表征学习的可行性。

Conclusion: 该工作是较早系统比较自监督学习技术在博弈策略表征学习中的研究之一，证明了即使是简单方法也能学到有用的策略嵌入。作者公开了代码以供后续研究扩展。

Abstract: We investigate the problem of learning useful policy representations (embeddings) in two-player zero-sum imperfect-information games. We make three contributions: First, we introduce methods of creating datasets of policies for a given game. Second, we propose methods to learn policy representations. Third, we introduce downstream tasks to evaluate the effectiveness of such representations.
  We evaluate each dataset method, embedding method, and downstream task on Kuhn and Leduc Poker. Although our methods are very basic, we demonstrate that useful behavioral representations are present in the learned embeddings. To our knowledge, this work is among the first to systematically compare self-supervised learning techniques for learning policy representations in games. Our code is available at https://github.com/VitamintK/ssl-project for others to extend.

</details>


### [208] [The risk of KV cache compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01520)
*Lukas Haverbeck,Carmen Amo Alonso,Andres Felipe Posada-Moreno,Sebastian Trimpe,Marco Pavone*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论角度刻画了KV缓存压缩的极小极大风险，揭示了压缩可行性的内在条件，并据此提出了具有理论保证的实用压缩算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer处理长序列时，softmax注意力机制需反复从大型KV缓存中读取，计算开销大。现有KV缓存压缩方法多依赖经验设计，缺乏系统理论指导——最坏情况下的不可能性结果已知，但在可压缩场景中如何设计算法仍无原则性框架。

Method: 作者以KV缓存的内在可压缩性为度量，刻画了KV缓存压缩的极小极大风险（minimax risk），揭示了何时以及如何实现精确压缩。在此基础上，提出面向因果掩码（causal masking）的KV缓存压缩设计原则，使其能高效映射到预填充（prefill）和自回归解码（autoregressive decoding）阶段，并达到极小极大最优风险。最终将这些原则实例化为一个实用算法。

Result: 理论层面，给出了KV缓存压缩的极小极大风险界限，建立了压缩可行性与缓存内在可压缩性之间的形式化关系。实验层面，所提出的算法在LongBench基准上表现良好。

Conclusion: 该工作为实用KV缓存压缩提供了一条具有理论保证的原则性路径，弥合了理论与经验设计之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Transformer inference on long sequences is expensive because softmax attention repeatedly reads from a large KV cache. The prevalent approach to this bottleneck is KV cache compression, which replaces the full cache with a compact summary. Despite its practical importance, the design of such summaries is largely driven by empirical experimentation. On the theoretical side, existing results show that KV cache compression can be impossible in the worst case, but offer little systematic guidance for designing algorithms in regimes where accurate compression is possible. We bridge this gap by characterizing the minimax risk of KV cache compression in terms of the intrinsic compressibility of a cache, revealing when and how accurate compression is possible. These results yield novel design principles for KV cache compression under causal masking that map efficiently to prefill and autoregressive decoding while achieving minimax-optimal risk. We instantiate these principles in a practical algorithm and report promising performance on LongBench in targeted experiments. Overall, our results provide a principled avenue for practical KV cache compression with theoretical guarantees.

</details>


### [209] [Multi-Head Recurrent Memory Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01523)
*Jiatong Li,Samuel Yeh,Sharon Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文诊断了循环记忆智能体在长上下文场景中的可靠性问题，发现性能瓶颈主要在于记忆保留而非记忆捕获。为此提出无需训练的多头循环记忆框架（MHM），通过将记忆分区为独立头并以阶段式"选择-更新"策略保护已保留内容，其实例 MHM-LRU 在 100K-1M token 范围内显著提升了保留率和端到端精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有循环记忆智能体虽可将 LLM 扩展至任意长上下文，但端到端性能随上下文增长而系统性退化。作者通过将性能分解为记忆捕获和记忆保留两个因素，定量确认保留是主导瓶颈——原因在于现有设计将记忆维护为单一文本块，每次更新都可能覆盖先前保留的内容。

Method: 提出 Multi-Head Recurrent Memory (MHM)，一个通用的、无需训练的记忆框架：将记忆划分为多个独立头，采用阶段式"选择-更新"策略，每步仅选择一个头进行更新，其余头在结构上被保护免于覆盖。作为轻量级实例，提出 Least-Recently-Updated MHM (MHM-LRU)，保证均匀的头利用率且零额外 token 开销。

Result: 在长上下文基准测试中，MHM-LRU 在 100K-1M token 范围内显著提升了记忆保留率和端到端准确率。在 RULER-HQA 的 896K token 上，记忆保留率从不足 30% 提升至 73.96%。这些增益跨模型家族、规模和任务类型均具有泛化性。

Conclusion: MHM 将保留负担从模型行为转移至架构设计，为构建可靠的长上下文循环记忆提供了实用且成本高效的路径，定位架构优化为这一方向的关键突破口。

Abstract: Recurrent memory agents extend LLMs to arbitrarily long contexts by iteratively consolidating input into a fixed-size memory window. Despite their scalability, these agents exhibit a well-documented reliability problem: end-to-end performance degrades systematically as context length grows. We diagnose this failure by decomposing performance into two factors--memory capture and memory retention--and quantitatively confirm that retention is the dominant bottleneck. Retention collapses because existing designs maintain memory as a monolithic text block, forcing every update to risk overwriting previously retained content. Motivated by this diagnosis, we propose Multi-Head Recurrent Memory (MHM), a general, training-free framework that partitions memory into independent heads governed by a stage-wise select-then-update strategy. At each step, exactly one head is selected for update while the remaining heads are structurally shielded from overwriting, shifting the burden of retention from model behavior to architectural design. As a lightweight instantiation, we introduce Least-Recently-Updated MHM (MHM-LRU), which guarantees uniform head utilization with zero additional token overhead. Extensive experiments on long-context benchmarks show that MHM-LRU substantially improves both retention and end-to-end accuracy across the 100K--1M token range, where baselines degrade sharply. On RULER-HQA at 896K tokens, MHM-LRU improves the memory retention rate from less than 30% to 73.96%. These gains generalize across model families, scales, and task types, positioning architectural optimization as a practical and cost-efficient path toward reliable long-context recurrent memory.

</details>


### [210] [Wind-Aware Reinforcement Learning Control of a Small Quadrotor Using Learned Onboard Wind Estimation in Simulated Atmospheric Turbulence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01528)
*Abdullah Al Tasim,Wei Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种两阶段学习框架：先用注意力增强的门控循环网络从机载运动学和动力学数据中估计局部风场，再将冻结的风估计器嵌入近端策略优化(PPO)强化学习飞行控制器，在湍流边界层中显著提升小型多旋翼飞行器的轨迹跟踪性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型多旋翼飞行器越来越多地在大气边界层中执行任务，该区域湍流风速与飞行器空速相当，严重降低轨迹跟踪精度，传统反馈控制难以应对。亟需一种能够感知并利用风场信息的高性能控制器。

Method: 两阶段学习流程：(1) 风估计器——注意力增强的门控循环网络(GRU)，在数千次模拟飞行数据上训练，模拟环境包含von Kármán湍流、幂律风切变和风向偏转；(2) 强化学习飞行控制器——采用PPO算法，接收冻结的风估计器输出作为额外观测输入，与仅依赖运动学信息的基线进行对比。通过三向消融实验将性能提升分解为运动学分量和风感知分量。

Result: 风估计器在未见风况下水平风矢量均方根误差为0.40 m/s，方向误差3.2°，垂直上升剖面的技能评分为0.861。PPO控制器在4-12 m/s平均风速下，水平轨迹跟踪误差相比无风感知的PD基线降低48%，评估回合胜率100%。消融实验表明风感知的贡献随风速增大而增加，在强风下约占总收益的一半。在13-15 m/s分布外强风下，控制器性能优雅退化，而基线彻底失效。

Conclusion: 结合显式风估计的强化学习控制器能显著提升湍流边界层中多旋翼飞行器的轨迹跟踪鲁棒性，风感知的收益随风速呈二次方尺度增长，且该方案对分布外风况具有良好的泛化退化特性。

Abstract: Small multirotor aircraft are increasingly tasked with operations in the atmospheric boundary layer, where turbulent winds comparable to the vehicle's airspeed degrade trajectory tracking and can defeat conventional feedback control. This work illustrates a two-stage learning pipeline that first estimates the local wind from onboard kinematics and dynamics and then exploits that estimate inside a reinforcement learning (RL) flight controller. The wind estimator, an attention-augmented gated recurrent network trained on thousands of simulated flights through von Karman turbulence with power-law shear and veer, recovers the horizontal wind vector with a per-flight root-mean-square error of 0.40 m/s and a direction error of 3.2 degrees on unseen wind regimes, an accuracy near the floor imposed by unresolved turbulence, and generalizes to vertical ascent profiles with a skill score of 0.861 over a constant-wind reference. A proximal policy optimization controller receiving the frozen estimator's output reduces horizontal trajectory tracking error by 48% relative to a wind-blind proportional-derivative baseline across mean winds of 4 m/s to 12 m/s, winning on 100% of evaluation episodes. A three-way ablation decomposes this improvement into a kinematic component, available without wind information, and a wind-perception component; the perception share rises with wind speed, from small in light winds toward roughly half the total benefit in strong winds, consistent with the quadratic scaling of aerodynamic drag. The controller degrades gracefully on out-of-distribution winds of 13 m/s to 15 m/s, where the baseline fails catastrophically.

</details>
