Abstract: Recent research has introduced distributed self-supervised learning (D-SSL) approaches to leverage vast amounts of unlabeled decentralized data. However, D-SSL faces the critical challenge of data heterogeneity, and there is limited theoretical understanding of how different D-SSL frameworks respond to this challenge. To fill this gap, we present a rigorous theoretical analysis of the robustness of D-SSL frameworks under non-IID (non-independent and identically distributed) settings. Our results show that pre-training with Masked Image Modeling (MIM) is inherently more robust to heterogeneous data than Contrastive Learning (CL), and that the robustness of decentralized SSL increases with average network connectivity, implying that federated learning (FL) is no less robust than decentralized learning (DecL). These findings provide a solid theoretical foundation for guiding the design of future D-SSL algorithms. To further illustrate the practical implications of our theory, we introduce MAR loss, a refinement of the MIM objective with local-to-global alignment regularization. Extensive experiments across model architectures and distributed settings validate our theoretical insights, and additionally confirm the effectiveness of MAR loss as an application of our analysis.

</details>


### [281] [Neuron-Aware Data Selection for Annotation-Free LLM Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02460)
*Zhuowei Chen,Xiang Lorraine Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Neuron-OPSD 是一种无需标注的自蒸馏框架，利用内部神经元激活来指导训练数据选择与教师上下文构建，通过 on-policy 蒸馏实现领域特化，同时保持跨域泛化能力并缓解校准误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前无标注自进化方法（如基于 SFT/GRPO 或奖励型 on-policy RL）存在两个核心问题：一是域外性能退化，二是校准误差膨胀。在专家标注成本高昂的专业领域中，亟需一种无需真实标签、无需在线交互反馈，且能同时保持域内性能和域外泛化能力的方法。

Method: 提出 Neuron-OPSD（Neuron On-Policy Self-Distillation），这是一个数据中心框架：1）利用模型内部神经元激活信号来筛选高质量训练数据；2）基于神经元激活构建教师上下文（teacher context）；3）通过 on-policy 蒸馏从教师分布中学习，全程无需真实标签。

Result: 在专业领域基准测试中，Neuron-OPSD 相比先前无标注基线方法，实现了域内任务性能提升，同时保持了跨域泛化能力，并有效缓解了校准坍塌问题。

Conclusion: Neuron-OPSD 为在线交互或外部监督成本高昂的场景提供了一种有效的无标注自蒸馏方案，概念上与依赖已记录、含奖励标签轨迹的离线 RL 方法有本质区别。

Abstract: Post-training large language models (LLMs) without real-world interaction feedback or human-labeled supervision remains challenging, particularly in specialized domains where expert annotations are costly to obtain. Recent annotation-free self-evolution methods address this by using the model's own outputs as supervision signals, constructing a teacher via additional context and aggregating predictions across multiple rollouts through majority voting to produce pseudo-labels. However, these approaches are not without drawbacks: SFT- and GRPO-based variants suffer out-of-domain performance degradation, while reward-based on-policy RL inflates calibration error. In this paper, we propose Neuron On-Policy Self-Distillation (Neuron-OPSD), a data-centric framework for annotation-free self-distillation that leverages internal neuron activations to guide both training-data selection and teacher context construction. The model is then trained via on-policy distillation from the teacher distribution, requiring no ground-truth labels at any stage. Across specialized-domain benchmarks, Neuron-OPSD improves in-domain task performance while preserving cross-domain generalization and mitigating calibration collapse over prior annotation-free baselines. This framework is particularly relevant to settings where online interaction or external supervision is costly or infeasible, and is conceptually distinct from offline RL approaches that rely on logged, reward-labeled trajectories.

</details>


### [282] [Beyond Adam: SOAP and Muon for Faster, Label-Efficient Training of Machine Learning Interatomic Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02499)
*Gil Harari,Yoel Zimmermann,Ola Tangen Kulseng,Laura Zichi,Chuin Wei Tan,Marc L. Descoteaux,Boris Kozinsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了矩阵结构优化器（Muon、SOAP、SOAP-Muon）与Adam在训练MLIP模型（NequIP、Allegro）上的表现，发现这些新型优化器在收敛速度和最终精度上均显著优于Adam，SOAP和SOAP-Muon表现尤为稳健，且在部分力监督场景下优势更为突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLIP领域在架构和数据集方面取得了长足进步，但优化器的选择长期被忽视，社区默认使用Adam及其变体。作者旨在探索矩阵结构优化器这一被忽略的设计维度，评估其是否能带来实质性性能提升。

Method: 选取NequIP和Allegro两种代表性MLIP模型作为测试平台，系统实现并对比了三类矩阵结构优化器（Muon、SOAP、SOAP-Muon）与Adam基线，在不同监督条件（包括部分力监督）下评估收敛速度和最终精度。

Result: 矩阵结构优化器在收敛速度和最终精度上均显著优于Adam；SOAP和SOAP-Muon表现最为稳健且一致；Muon仅提供相对Adam的部分增益；在部分力监督设置下，优势进一步放大。

Conclusion: 优化器选择是MLIP训练中一个被忽视但影响显著的设计维度，矩阵结构优化器尤其是SOAP系列方法有潜力成为Adam的有力替代方案，值得社区重视和进一步探索。

Abstract: Machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) have become a hallmark of AI for scientific simulation. While efforts on new architectures and datasets have led to increasingly accurate and general models, the choice of optimizer for training has largely remained unexplored, defaulting to Adam and its variants in the community. Here, we implement and systematically compare a class of recently proposed matrix-structured optimizers, including Muon, SOAP, and the hybrid SOAP-Muon, for training NequIP and Allegro MLIP models. We find that these optimizers can substantially outperform Adam in both convergence speed and final accuracy. SOAP and SOAP-Muon emerge as robust and consistently strong methods, while Muon only provides partial gains relative to Adam. The improvements are particularly pronounced under partial force supervision. Our results indicate that optimizer choice is an overlooked yet impactful design axis for MLIPs.

</details>


### [283] [DemoPSD: Disagreement-Modulated Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02502)
*Yunhe Li,Hao Shi,Wenhao Liu,Mengzhe Ruan,Hanxu Hou,Zhongxiang Dai,Shuang Qiu,Linqi Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DemoPSD 提出了一种选择性采纳教师指导的在线自蒸馏框架，通过构建教师与学生分布的反向KL重心目标来平衡知识学习与推理能力保留，有效缓解了特权信息泄露和探索能力抑制问题，在科学推理基准上优于GRPO和SDPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线自蒸馏方法（如OPSD）中，教师模型基于特权信息的密集token级监督会导致三大问题：(1) 对域内模式的过拟合；(2) 抑制探索能力，损害跨域泛化；(3) 特权信息泄露——学生模型编码了测试时不可用的答案依赖捷径。这些问题本质上源于学生盲目拟合完整的教师分布，缺乏对教师指导的选择性采纳机制。

Method: 提出DemoPSD框架，核心思想是选择性采纳教师指导。具体而言：不让学生直接拟合教师分布，而是引导学生朝向一个"反向KL重心目标"——教师分布与学生分布的加权几何组合。通过衡量教师与学生分布之间的差异（discrepancy），在每token位置自适应控制混合权重。该方法在理论上被证明具有两个性质：(1) 泄露衰减（leakage attenuation）：有效缓解特权信息泄露；(2) 探索保留（exploration preservation）：在密集token级蒸馏下保持探索能力。

Result: 在SciKnowEval基准（涵盖四个科学领域）上的广泛实验表明，DemoPSD在性能上优于GRPO和SDPO，同时保持更高的训练熵值，并能稳健泛化到分布外的GPQA基准测试。

Conclusion: DemoPSD通过反向KL重心目标和自适应混合策略，成功解决了在线自蒸馏中特权信息泄露与探索抑制两个根本性问题，既吸收了教师知识又保留了学生自主推理能力，在域内和域外评测中均表现出色。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) has emerged as a practical method for training large language models (LLMs) to reason, where a single model acts as both the teacher and the student with different levels of information access. However, recent studies have found that the teacher's dense token-level supervision, conditioned on privileged information, can lead to overfitting to in-domain patterns, suppress exploration, and hurt cross-domain generalization, while also introducing a more fundamental issue: *privileged information leakage*, where the student encodes answer-dependent shortcuts that are unavailable at test time. We introduce **DemoPSD**, a novel framework that resolves such problems through the idea of *selective adoption of teacher guidance*. Instead of fitting the full teacher distribution, DemoPSD steers the student toward a *reverse-KL barycenter target*, a weighted geometric combination of the teacher and student distributions, that naturally balances learning from the teacher with preserving the student's own reasoning capacity. We measure the difference between their distributions and use such a discrepancy to adaptively control the blending at each token position. We provably show that DemoPSD achieves **(1)** *leakage attenuation*, i.e., effective mitigation of privileged information leakage; and **(2)** *exploration preservation*, i.e., preservation of exploration capacity under dense token-level distillation. Extensive experiments on SciKnowEval across four scientific fields show that DemoPSD outperforms both GRPO and SDPO while maintaining higher training entropy and robustly generalizing to out-of-distribution GPQA benchmarks.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [284] [Full Bayesian Reinforcement Learning via LF-IBIS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01741)
*Stefano Masini,Cecilia Viscardi,Michela Baccini*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: LF-IBIS 是一种无需似然函数的贝叶斯强化学习算法，通过结合近似贝叶斯计算与迭代批量重要性采样，在环境动态缺乏显式或可处理似然的情况下实现完整的贝叶斯推断，同时提供环境参数和最优策略的后验分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数贝叶斯强化学习方法需要显式的似然函数，但在现实场景中，环境动态的似然函数往往不可获取或难以处理，这严重限制了 BRL 的适用性。

Method: 提出 LF-IBIS 算法，将近似贝叶斯计算（ABC）与迭代批量重要性采样（IBIS）相结合，随着新交互数据的到来在线更新智能体的信念，无需显式似然函数即可实现对环境参数和最优策略的近似后验推断。

Result: 在临床试验的响应适应性随机化仿真研究中验证了方法的有效性（该场景下存在闭式后验可供验证）；在额外实验中进一步展示了后验无闭式情形下的在线策略更新能力。

Conclusion: LF-IBIS 成功在无似然设定下实现了贝叶斯强化学习的在线信念更新，为探索-利用权衡的贝叶斯处理提供了策略不确定性量化，拓展了 BRL 在实际应用中的可行性。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) is a sequential decision-making framework in which an agent learns optimal policies through interaction with an environment by maximizing cumulative rewards. Among RL methods, Bayesian Reinforcement Learning (BRL) addresses common practical challenges related to data scarcity by leveraging prior knowledge about the environment and sequential belief updates. However, most BRL approaches require an explicit likelihood function, which is frequently inaccessible or intractable in real-world settings.
  We propose Likelihood-Free Iterated Batch Importance Sampling (LF-IBIS), a novel algorithm for BRL that updates the agent's beliefs online as new interactions become available. By combining Approximate Bayesian Computation with Iterated Batch Importance Sampling, LF-IBIS enables full Bayesian inference in settings where the environment dynamics are not described by an explicit or tractable likelihood. The method yields approximate posterior distributions over both environment parameters and optimal policies, providing a quantification of policy uncertainty useful for a Bayesian treatment of the exploration-exploitation trade-off. We test the method on a simulation study in response-adaptive randomization in clinical trials, where closed-form posteriors enable validation. Additional experiments address settings where the posterior has no closed form and illustrate online policy updating based on the posterior distribution of the optimal policy.

</details>


### [285] [Statistical Properties of $k$-means Clustering for Data Missing Completely at Random](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01945)
*Xin Guan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了缺失数据下k-means聚类的统计性质，建立了聚类中心估计的√n-超额风险界和相合性，并在MCAR机制下证明了√n-收敛速度与渐近正态性，揭示了高维场景下面临的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典k-means无法直接用于不完整数据，现有方法多聚焦于实践精度提升，缺乏渐近理论保证。本文旨在填补这一空白，从统计理论角度为缺失数据k-means聚类提供严格的理论基础。

Method: 在一般缺失机制下建立√n-超额风险界并证明聚类中心相合性；针对MCAR机制进一步推导聚类中心的√n-收敛速率和渐近正态性；分析不完全数据估计的中心收敛到真实全数据中心的充分条件（涉及缺失概率和真实聚类间距）。

Result: 1）一般缺失机制下获得√n-超额风险界和聚类中心相合性；2）MCAR下得到√n-收敛速率和渐近正态性；3）发现MCAR下达到√n速率和收敛到真实中心均要求k个真实中心在每个维度上都互异，揭示高维场景中的严峻挑战；4）合成数据数值模拟验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 本文为缺失数据k-means聚类提供了完整的渐近理论保障，明确了缺失概率与聚类分离度之间的充分条件，同时指出了高维应用中的重大挑战，为后续研究指明了方向。

Abstract: The classical $k$-means clustering cannot be directly used to incomplete data, and existing $k$-means-based clustering for missing data primarily focus on improving the practical accuracy of clustering, whereas most of them lack theoretical guarantees in the asymptotic sense. In this paper, we investigate the statistical properties of $k$-means clustering in the presence of missing data. We first establish the $\sqrt{n}$-excess risk bound and prove the consistency of the estimated cluster centers under general missing mechanisms. For the Missing Completely at Random (MCAR) mechanism, we further derive the $\sqrt{n}$-convergence rate and asymptotic normality of the estimated cluster centers. Moreover, we study in what cases the cluster centers estimated by incomplete data converge to the true cluster centers of original fully observed data, and give a sufficient condition about the missing probability and the separation among true clusters. These results provide a theoretical guarantee for missing-data-$k$-means. Notably, our analysis reveal that under MCAR mechanism, both achieving the $\sqrt{n}$-rate and converging to the true cluster centers require $k$ true centers to be distinct in every dimension, highlighting the significant challenges of application in high-dimensional regimes. Finally, we conduct numerical simulations on synthetic incomplete datasets to support our theoretical analysis results.

</details>


### [286] [Autorelevance function and other feature relevance measures for univariate time series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01959)
*Julian Cardenas,Jamie Arjona,Pedro Delicado*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种与模型无关的方法论，用于衡量单变量时间序列预测模型中各滞后项的相关性。通过结合Ghost变量、Shapley值及加性重要性度量，作者引入了自相关性和偏自相关性函数作为滞后重要性指标，并提出用模型自身的一步预测来替代联盟方法中的缺失特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在时间序列预测中，理解各滞后项对预测的贡献至关重要，但传统自相关和偏自相关函数无法反映模型内部对滞后项的实际利用情况。作者旨在填补这一空白，开发一种模型无关的滞后相关性度量方法，以揭示任意预测模型（包括黑箱模型）对滞后结构的依赖模式。

Method: 1. 将Ghost变量框架与Shapley值结合，定义auto-relevance和partial auto-relevance函数作为滞后重要性度量；2. 提出一种新颖的缺失特征替代方法——使用同一模型的一步预测值填补联盟方法中的缺失特征，以更贴合时间序列的时序依赖特性；3. 在季节性ARMA族模型和循环神经网络上，通过仿真数据和真实数据对上述方法进行验证。

Result: 计算得到的相关性度量在几乎所有测试案例中均成功展示了预期的滞后结构，证明了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的结合Ghost变量、Shapley值与加性重要性度量的框架，能够有效揭示时间序列预测模型中各滞后的真实重要性，且具有良好的模型无关性和广泛的适用性。

Abstract: We propose a model agnostic methodology to measure lag relevance in machine learning forecasting models applied to univariate time series. Particularly, we are working in the context of time series using the frameworks of Ghost variables and Shapley values, together with additive importance measures, to introduce the auto-relevance and partial auto-relevance functions as the lag importance values. Additionally, we propose a novel method to replace absent features in coalition based methods with a one step forecast from the same model. We evaluate these proposals under different simulations and real data cases. This combined framework perspective is particularly suitable for time series. In addition, to show our discoveries we use a pull of models from the seasonal ARMA family and recurrent neural networks. We found that the calculated relevance measures successfully demonstrate the expected lag structure in almost all cases.

</details>


### [287] [Prediction Sets for Counterfactual Decisions: Coverage, Optimality, and Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02206)
*Yurui Zheng,Ying Jin*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向反事实决策的决策论框架，引入"策略耦合覆盖"这一新概念作为不确定性与行动之间的最优接口。基于此，作者开发了策略耦合风险规避共形预测（PC-RACP）方法，能够在保证有限样本覆盖有效性的同时实现更高的决策效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前不确定性量化方法（如共形预测）只关注预测集的统计有效性，忽视了在反事实场景中——即结果取决于所采取的行动时——不确定性与决策规则之间的耦合关系。统计有效性的保证无法直接转化为最优决策，填补这一缺口是本文的核心动机。

Method: 作者首先提出"策略耦合覆盖"概念，定义为在预测集自身所诱导的行动下对实际结果的平均覆盖。基于此，建立了一个决策论框架：证明max-min决策规则在分布模糊性下是极小化极大最优的；证明优化策略耦合覆盖等价于更强的通用覆盖形式以及直接的风险规避策略优化；最后给出两阶段程序PC-RACP，用共形预测技术在有限样本下逼近最优预测集，并提供严格的覆盖保证。

Result: 理论层面，建立了策略耦合覆盖、通用覆盖与风险规避策略优化三者之间的等价性，并给出了总体最优预测集的显式形式。实践层面，模拟实验和真实的电子邮件营销实验均表明，PC-RACP在保持有效覆盖的前提下，比现有方法获得了更高的效用，且忽视反事实决策结构对有效性和效用均不利。

Conclusion: 策略耦合覆盖是连接不确定性和反事实决策的最优且无损的接口。PC-RACP方法在实际决策场景中兼具理论严谨性与实践优越性，忽视反事实结构会导致有效性与效用的双重损失。

Abstract: Predictions are increasingly used to guide high-stakes decisions, from treatment selection to policy making. To ensure reliability with imperfect predictions, uncertainty quantification methods such as conformal prediction build prediction sets with coverage guarantees. However, statistical validity alone does not immediately determine the decisions to take, nor the optimality thereof. This gap is especially delicate in counterfactual settings where the outcome that materializes depends on the action taken, so uncertainty cannot be specified independently of the decision rule.
  We develop a decision-theoretic framework for uncertainty-informed counterfactual decisions. We identify a novel notion of \emph{policy-coupled coverage} -- namely, coverage of the realized outcome under the action induced by the prediction sets themselves -- as the optimal and lossless interface between uncertainty and action. It plays three roles. First, it justifies acting via a natural max-min rule as minimax-optimal under distributional ambiguity. Second, optimizing prediction sets under policy-coupled coverage is equivalent both to a stronger universal-coverage formulation and to the direct risk-averse optimization over policies and utility certificates; this equivalence yields the explicit form of the population-optimal prediction sets. Third, it admits a two-stage procedure, Policy-Coupled Risk-Averse Conformal Prediction (PC-RACP), that approximates these optimal sets with rigorous finite-sample coverage. Simulations and a real email-marketing experiment confirm that PC-RACP delivers higher utility than existing approaches while maintaining valid coverage, and that ignoring the counterfactual structure of the decision problem is suboptimal for both validity and utility.

</details>


### [288] [An Additive MLP-GNN Framework for Characterizing Chemical and Structural Contributions to Aqueous Solubility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02212)
*Sampreeti Bhattacharya,Arkaprava Roy*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出一种加性深度学习框架，将物理化学描述符（化学分支）与分子图拓扑（结构分支）分离编码，仅在预测阶段通过加性模型（可选乘性交互）组合，实现化学与结构贡献的透明分解，并在两个溶解度数据集上取得竞争性预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有溶解度预测模型大多将物理化学描述符与分子图信息融合为单一表征，导致无法区分预测是由全局化学性质、分子结构还是两者共同驱动，缺乏可解释性。因此需要一种能够清晰分离并量化这两类信息各自贡献的框架。

Method: 提出加性深度学习框架：物理化学描述符由多层感知机（化学分支）编码，分子图拓扑由图神经网络（结构分支）编码，两分支输出通过加性模型（含可选乘性交互项）在预测阶段组合。训练策略上，先在较大的AqSolDB数据集上预训练，再在较小的BigSolDB2数据集上微调。事后解释采用分支输出的最佳线性投影、不同溶解度类别的分子级嵌入汇总，以及基于GNNExplainer的原子级掩码在官能团层面聚合分析。

Result: 在AqSolDB和BigSolDB2两个数据集上均取得竞争性预测性能，预训练-微调策略显著提升准确度并降低运行间变异。化学分支与已知物理化学描述符对齐，结构分支捕获了与溶解度相关的图拓扑和官能团模式。

Conclusion: 该框架在保持竞争性预测精度的同时，使化学信息与结构信息的各自角色更加透明可解释，为药物发现早期阶段的溶解度预测提供了一种更具洞察力的建模方法。

Abstract: Aqueous solubility is a key property in early-stage drug discovery, but most predictive models merge physicochemical descriptors and molecular graph information into a single representation, obscuring whether a prediction is driven by global chemistry, molecular structure, or both. We present an additive deep-learning framework that keeps these two sources of information separate throughout training: physicochemical descriptors are encoded by a multilayer perceptron (the chemical branch) and molecular graph topology by a graph neural network (the structural branch), with the two outputs combined only at the prediction stage through an additive model with an optional multiplicative interaction. This design provides a direct decomposition of chemical and structural components that can be examined separately after training. Furthermore, pretraining on the larger AqSolDB dataset and fine-tuning on the smaller BigSolDB2 dataset substantially improve accuracy and reduce run-to-run variations, indicating generalizability of the learned features from the data-rich settings. We further interpret the fitted model using best linear projections of the branch outputs, molecule-level embedding summaries across solubility classes, and atom-level GNNExplainer masks aggregated over functional groups. These analyses show that the chemical branch aligns with familiar physicochemical descriptors, while the structural branch captures graph-topological and functional-group patterns associated with solubility. Across both datasets, the framework attains competitive predictive performance while making the distinct roles of chemical and structural information more transparent.

</details>


### [289] [The Dual Nature of LLM Persona: Aggregated Tendencies and Frame-Dependent Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02368)
*Yuan Yuan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文通过操纵问卷题目顺序，研究LLM人格表达中聚合特征与几何特征的双重性质，发现聚合特征（大五得分）对框架鲁棒但易退化，而SPD流形上的几何特征依赖框架对齐，在共享框架下可恢复并超越聚合特征，揭示了LLM人格的框架依赖性本质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于心理测量问卷评估LLM人格的方法通常仅依赖聚合分数，丢弃了实例内相关结构。作者质疑这种几何结构是内在固有的还是依赖于问卷框架的，旨在揭示LLM人格表达中被聚合方法忽视的信息维度。

Method: 使用IPIP-50问卷，在GPT-4o上模拟美国人和华裔美国人两种人格。通过操纵问题顺序构建实例内相关矩阵，在对称正定（SPD）流形上分析几何结构。比较聚合特征（大五分数）与几何特征在不同框架（原始顺序、随机打乱顺序）下的表现变化。

Result: 聚合特征在随机化下退化21%但对框架鲁棒；几何特征在框架不对齐时崩溃42%，但在共享框架下恢复至84%，超过聚合特征的76%。这种崩溃-恢复模式表明人格几何并非内在固有，而是框架依赖的协调模式。

Conclusion: LLM人格表达具有双重性质：框架依赖的几何结构与框架鲁棒的聚合特征。这要求采用框架感知的评估方法，并对将LLM人格视为静态特质的传统观念构成挑战。

Abstract: Evaluations of LLM personas via psychometric questionnaires typically rely on aggregate scores, discarding within-instance correlation structure. We test whether this geometric structure is intrinsic or frame-dependent. Constructing within-instance correlation matrices from IPIP-50 responses, we analyze geometry on SPD manifolds under manipulated question orderings in GPT-4o simulating American and Chinese-American personas. We find that persona expression comprises two dissociable components: aggregated features (Big Five scores) degrade under randomization (21% drop) but are frame-robust; geometric features (SPD manifold) collapse under frame misalignment (42% drop) but recover substantially (to 84%) under shared frames, surpassing aggregated features (76%). This collapse-recovery pattern reveals that persona geometry is not intrinsic but a frame-dependent coordination pattern encoding information invisible to aggregation. Our findings establish a dual-nature framework for LLM personas, frame-dependent geometry versus frame-robust aggregates, necessitating frame-aware evaluation and challenging static trait conceptions.

</details>


### [290] [eXact-Prior Variational Autoencoder (X-VAE): Learning Data-Adaptive Gaussian Mixture Priors for Latent Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01275)
*Qijun Chen,Shaofan Li*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: X-VAE 提出用预训练自编码器的隐空间经验分布替代标准正态先验，引入隐变量缩放因子实现对生成样本多样性与保真度的显式控制，在保持重建质量的同时提升隐表示与真实数据分布的对齐程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 VAE 的标准各向同性高斯先验无法捕捉复杂数据集的真实隐分布，导致重建精度下降、样本质量降低、隐空间表达能力受限。

Method: 利用预训练 AE 的隐编码经验均值和标准差参数化一个数据自适应的 Gaussian 先验，替换标准正态先验；引入隐变量缩放因子（latent scaling factor）以显式控制采样方差，从而调节多样性与保真度的平衡；并推导了相应的 KL 散度目标函数。

Result: 在标准基准数据集上，X-VAE 保持了重建质量，同时隐表示更好地对齐经验数据分布，生成样本更具可控性和真实感。

Conclusion: X-VAE 通过数据自适应先验和缩放因子机制，有效弥合了先验与真实隐分布之间的 mismatch，特别适用于工业与工程设计等需要满足严格结构或功能约束的场景。

Abstract: Variational Autoencoders (VAEs) commonly assume a standard isotropic Gaussian prior over the latent space, an assumption that often fails to capture the true distribution of latent representations for complex datasets. This mismatch can limit reconstruction accuracy, reduce sample quality, and constrain the expressive power of the learned latent space. We propose the eXact-Prior Variational Autoencoder (X-VAE), a framework that replaces the conventional standard normal prior with a Gaussian prior derived from the latent representations of a pretrained autoencoder (AE). Specifically, the empirical mean and standard deviation of the AE latent codes are used to parameterize a data-adaptive prior that more closely reflects the underlying structure of the training data. During generation, X-VAE introduces a latent scaling factor that enables explicit control over the variance of the sampled latent vectors, providing a simple mechanism for balancing sample diversity and fidelity. This flexibility makes the proposed approach particularly well suited for applications such as industrial and engineering design, where generated solutions must satisfy strict structural or functional constraints while still permitting meaningful design exploration. We present the mathematical formulation of well-suited X-VAE, derive the corresponding KL divergence objective for the proposed prior, and evaluate the method on standard benchmark datasets. Experimental results demonstrate that X-VAE preserves reconstruction quality while producing latent representations that better align with the empirical data distribution, leading to improved controllability and more realistic generated samples.

</details>


### [291] [Born Discrete, Made Smooth: Variational Formulation of Shallow Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02003)
*Matej Benko,Pierre Bousquet,Iwona Chlebicka,Błażej Miasojedow*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文提出将浅层神经网络的离散训练问题转化为连续变分代理问题，在加权Sobolev空间中定义了一族λ-凸泛函，证明其全局适定性和几乎C³正则性，并进一步证明最优参数密度可通过求解单个线性系统获得，无需迭代优化，同时给出了推广误差界和有限宽度网络收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经网络优化理论面临两大困境：(1) 非凸景观和随机启发式方法使得优化原理在理论上难以捉摸；(2) 基于Wasserstein或平均场的方法存在正则性受限和离散化困难。作者旨在从根本上重构问题，用连续变分框架替代离散训练，以获得更好的正则性和凸分析工具。

Method: 作者在加权Sobolev空间上构造了一族参数密度上的λ-凸泛函，利用椭圆正则性理论和凸分析工具，将最优参数密度的求解归结为单个线性系统的求解，完全绕过迭代优化。通过变分法建立了连续最优解与有限宽度网络之间的桥梁。

Result: (1) 变分问题全局适定、稳定，且解具有几乎C³正则性；(2) 最优参数密度可通过求解单个线性系统获得；(3) 推广误差以1/α速率（相对于正则化参数）受控；(4) 宽度为N的有限网络以O(1/N)速率收敛到连续最优解。

Conclusion: 该连续变分框架为理解过参数化提供了基于变分微积分的统一理论视角，弥合了神经正切核（NTK）机制与特征学习机制之间的鸿沟，为神经网络优化奠定了更坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: Although neural networks are remarkably effective, their underlying optimization principles remain theoretically elusive, often characterized by non-convex landscapes and stochastic heuristics. In this work, we propose a paradigm shift by replacing the discrete training problem of shallow neural networks with a well-posed continuum variational surrogate. We identify a family of $λ$-convex functionals over parameter densities in weighted Sobolev spaces and prove that these variational problems are globally well-posed, stable, and exhibit unexpected almost $C^3$ regularity.
  Unlike existing Wasserstein-based or Mean-Field approaches, which often face limited regularity and discretization challenges, our formulation provides direct access to elliptic regularity and convex analysis. This allows us to prove that the optimal parameter density can be obtained by solving a single linear system, bypassing iterative optimization entirely. We establish explicit generalization error controls at a rate of $1/α$ relative to the regularization parameter, and prove that finite-width networks of size $N$ achieve the continuum optimum at an $O(1/N)$ rate. This perspective bridges the gap between the Neural Tangent Kernel (NTK) and feature-learning regimes, providing a principled framework for understanding over-parameterization through the lens of variational calculus.

</details>


<div id='stat.ME'></div>

# stat.ME [[Back]](#toc)

### [292] [J- and MJ-Type Tests for Non-Nested Parametric Survival Models with a Cure Fraction: A Score Test Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01379)
*Cynthia A. V. Tojeiro,Francisco Cribari-Neto,Tatiene C. Souza,Tarciana L. Pereira*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于得分检验的规范检验方法，用于区分具有治愈分数的非嵌套参数生存模型。该方法通过增强原假设对数似然并采用参数自助法估计KL偏差，构建了MJ统计量，可检验"至少有一个候选模型被正确指定"的全局原假设，同时提供模型选择准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在含治愈分数的生存分析中，不同参数模型（仅基线分布不同）常为非嵌套关系，传统的模型选择方法（如AIC）缺乏正式的假设检验框架来判别哪个模型是真实的数据生成过程。作者旨在填补这一空白，提供一个既能进行统计推断又能辅助模型选择的统一方法。

Method: 核心方法是将原假设模型的对数似然用竞争模型的信息进行增强，并施加得分检验以判断额外信息的冗余性。检验仅需受限最大似然估计，无需拟合增强模型。两模型情形下，得分统计量退化为个体对数似然差值的样本均值的二次型，其带符号平方根与Vuong检验统计量一致。区别于Vuong检验：(1) 检验特定模型为真实数据生成过程的原假设；(2) 采用无符号统计量，自然推广至M≥2个竞争模型；(3) 通过参数自助法估计Kullback-Leibler偏差。最终构建的MJ统计量综合多个个体J检验，评估全局原假设。

Result: 在两模型情形下，所提得分检验的带符号平方根与Vuong检验统计量重合，验证了方法的理论合理性。MJ统计量能够处理多个竞争模型的全局检验问题，并兼具模型选择功能。

Conclusion: 所提出的规范检验方法为含治愈分数的非嵌套参数生存模型提供了一个严密的推断框架，既能在统计上检验模型是否正确指定，又能在多个候选模型中给出选择依据，其参数自助法估计KL偏差的策略进一步增强了方法的实用性。

Abstract: We propose specification tests for discriminating among non-nested parametric survival models with a cure fraction, focusing on models that differ only in their baseline distributions. The proposed approach augments the null log-likelihood with information from competing models and applies a score test to assess whether the additional information is redundant. Because the test relies only on restricted maximum likelihood estimates, it avoids fitting augmented models. For two competing models, the score statistic reduces to a quadratic form in the sample mean of the individual log-likelihood differences. We show that its signed square root coincides with Vuong's test statistic, although our framework differs in three important respects: it tests the specific null hypothesis that a given model is the true data-generating process, it uses an unsigned statistic that extends naturally to $M \ge 2$ competing models, and it estimates the Kullback-Leibler bias by parametric bootstrap. The resulting MJ statistic combines the individual J tests to assess the global null hypothesis that at least one candidate model is correctly specified, while also providing a model-selection criterion.

</details>


### [293] [MATCH: Multiplier-Assisted Tests for Conditional Hypotheses in Non-Euclidean Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02295)
*Leheng Cai,Xu Guo,Qirui Hu*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: MATCH 提出了一种基于样本分割与随机乘子的统一检验框架，用于在 Fréchet 回归中检验非欧数据的条件假设，涵盖全局显著性、部分显著性和模型充分性检验，解决了原假设下损失差一阶退化的关键难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 Fréchet 回归中，检验非欧数据是否匹配目标模型时，传统的 held-out 损失差在原假设下是一阶退化的——oracle 损失重合，plug-in 统计量被冗余估计误差主导。现有方法需要残差或切空间坐标，缺乏统一的非退化检验框架。

Method: 提出 MATCH 程序：利用样本分割将数据分为训练集和保持集，在保持集损失上施加独立随机乘子，构造出具有非退化高斯主项（无需残差或切空间坐标）的检验统计量。进一步提出交叉拟合检验和重复交叉拟合结合 p 值合并，以提升数据利用效率和稳定性。

Result: 理论上建立了原假设下的渐近有效性、固定备择假设下的一致性以及局部功效保证。模拟实验在分布响应、对称正定矩阵响应和球面响应上验证了理论结果。应用分析了县级家庭收入分布和北大西洋热带气旋位置数据，展示了方法的实用性。

Conclusion: MATCH 为 Fréchet 回归中的条件假设检验提供了一个统一、灵活且理论完备的框架，有效避免了退化问题，在非欧数据分析中具有广泛的适用性和良好的有限样本表现。

Abstract: We propose a new procedure MATCH (Multiplier-Assisted Tests for Conditional Hypotheses) to test whether the non-Euclidean data match the target model, which is a general framework for significance and specification testing in Fréchet regression. MATCH covers global significance, partial significance, and the adequacy of global Fréchet regression, providing a unified way to compare unrestricted conditional Fréchet means with restricted alternatives. One of the key challenges is that the ordinary held-out loss difference is first-order degenerate under the null: the oracle losses coincide, and plug-in statistics is dominated by nuisance estimation error. MATCH uses sample splitting and independent random multipliers on held-out losses to create a nondegenerate Gaussian leading term without residuals or tangent-space coordinates. To improve data use and stability, we further develop cross-fitted tests and repeated cross-fitting with p-value merging. We establish asymptotic null validity, consistency under fixed alternatives, and local power guarantees. Simulations for distributional, symmetric positive-definite (SPD) matrix-valued, and spherical responses support the theoretical findings, and applications to county-level household income distributions and North Atlantic tropical-cyclone locations demonstrate the practical use of the proposed tests.

</details>


### [294] [Cross-Audit Projection for Model Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02328)
*Yijian Huang*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 针对二分类模型中K折交叉验证在估计类别特定风险时表现不佳（甚至不如经验风险估计）的现象，本文通过高阶渐近分析揭示了其原因，并提出了一种名为cross-audit projection (CAP)的两步估计方法，该方法兼具一阶渐近等价于经验风险与二阶渐近无偏的优良性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: K折交叉验证被广泛用于缓解经验风险估计中的过度乐观问题，但在二分类的经验风险最小化场景下，数值实验发现K折CV在估计类别特定风险时可能表现很差，甚至比经验估计更差。这一反直觉现象需要理论解释，并需要提出更好的模型风险预测方法。

Method: 作者首先进行高阶渐近分析，揭示K折CV收敛速率可能更慢，而经验估计存在二阶渐近偏差（导致过度乐观）。基于此，提出两阶段CAP方法：(1) Cross-audit步骤：采用与K折CV相同的重抽样方案在子样本中估计过度乐观程度；(2) Projection步骤：基于渐近理论对经验风险进行偏差校正时调整缩减的样本量。同时开发了配套的推断程序。

Result: 理论结果表明，CAP估计量一阶渐近等价于经验风险，且具有二阶渐近无偏性。模拟研究表明CAP在有限样本下具备良好的性能优势，在乳腺癌检测的实际应用中进一步验证了该方法。

Conclusion: K折交叉验证在估计类别特定风险时存在严重缺陷，本文提出的CAP方法通过结合重抽样与渐近理论，成功实现了经验风险的一阶渐近等价与二阶无偏性，为模型风险预测提供了更可靠的替代方案。

Abstract: For training-data-based model risk prediction, $K$-fold cross-validation~(CV) is widely used to mitigate the well-known over-optimism of the empirical risk and is often regarded as reliable. However, for binary classification via empirical risk minimization, our numerical studies reveal a surprising phenomenon: $K$-fold CV may perform poorly in estimating class-specific risks, even worse than the empirical estimator. We perform a higher-order asymptotic analysis showing that $K$-fold CV may converge at a slower rate, whereas the empirical estimator exhibits a second-order asymptotic bias that explains its over-optimism. These findings motivate a novel two-step procedure for model risk prediction, termed cross-audit projection (CAP). The cross-audit step adopts the same resampling scheme as $K$-fold CV to estimate over-optimism in subsamples, while the asymptotic-theory-informed projection step adjusts for the reduced sample size in bias correction of the empirical risk. The resulting CAP estimator is first-order asymptotically equivalent to the empirical risk while achieving second-order asymptotic unbiasedness. An accompanying inference procedure is also developed. Simulation studies support theoretical advantages of CAP and demonstrate favorable finite-sample performance. An application to breast cancer detection further illustrates the proposed method.

</details>


### [295] [Merging of Bayes and quasi-Bayes empirical Bayes procedures for Poisson compound decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02340)
*Stefano Favaro,Sandra Fortini*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文从g-modeling视角研究泊松复合决策问题，比较了基于Dirichlet过程后验的贝叶斯经验贝叶斯策略与基于Newton算法的拟贝叶斯策略。作者建立了两种估计的边际概率质量函数的集中速率，并将其转化为相应遗憾的衰减速率，证明了两策略的频率学派合并结果，并将分析推广到多维情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 泊松复合决策问题是统计学中长期存在的经典问题。拟贝叶斯策略（基于Newton算法）在计算上具有吸引力，但其与贝叶斯策略（基于Dirichlet过程后验）之间的理论关系缺乏严格论证。本文旨在为两种非参数g-modeling策略提供理论比较与统一框架。

Method: 在具有"真实"（oracle）混合分布的泊松混合模型下，作者首先推导贝叶斯和拟贝叶斯估计所诱导的边际概率质量函数的集中速率；然后将这些速率转化为对应遗憾（解释为超额贝叶斯风险）的衰减速率；最后证明贝叶斯与拟贝叶斯经验贝叶斯策略之间的频率学派合并结果，并将分析扩展至多维泊松复合决策问题。

Result: 理论上，建立了两种策略的边际概率质量函数集中速率，并给出了遗憾衰减速率及两者之间的频率学派合并定理。数值实验表明，拟贝叶斯策略在达到与贝叶斯策略相当的精度的同时，所需计算资源显著减少，在多维情形下计算优势尤为突出。

Conclusion: 拟贝叶斯经验贝叶斯策略在理论上与贝叶斯策略具有频率学派合并性，统计性能相当，但计算效率显著更优，特别适用于多维泊松复合决策问题。

Abstract: The Poisson compound decision problem is a long-standing problem in statistics, in which empirical Bayes methods are used to estimate Poisson means under a mixture model. We study this problem from the viewpoint of $g$-modeling, comparing two nonparametric strategies for estimating the unknown mixing distribution: a Bayesian empirical Bayes strategy, based on the Dirichlet process posterior, and a quasi-Bayesian empirical Bayes strategy, based on Newton's algorithm. The latter is computationally attractive, but its relationship with the Bayesian strategy requires theoretical justification. Under a Poisson mixture model with a ``true'', or oracle, mixing distribution, we establish concentration rates for the marginal probability mass functions induced by the Bayesian and quasi-Bayesian estimates. These rates are then translated into rates of decay for the corresponding regrets, interpreted as excess Bayes risks, and used to prove a frequentist merging result between the Bayesian and quasi-Bayesian empirical Bayes strategies. We also extend the analysis to the multidimensional Poisson compound decision problem. Numerical experiments on synthetic data illustrate that the quasi-Bayesian strategy achieves accuracy comparable to the Bayesian strategy, while requiring substantially fewer computational resources, especially in the multidimensional setting.

</details>


### [296] [Inference for Group Interaction Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02385)
*Jiawei Fu,Cyrus Samii,Ye Wang*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文为"群体交互实验"（个体被随机分配到群体中，群体接受不同处理条件）建立了基于设计的推断框架。作者区分了群体固定/随机形成、潜在结果固定/受干扰等多种情景，在稀疏抽样渐近体系下证明了聚类稳健推断在存在干扰时仍能提供有效的推断，并提出了新颖的耦合策略用于推导常用估计量的渐近分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在社会科学和经济学实验中，常见的实验设计是先将个体随机分配到群体中，再对群体施加不同的处理条件。这种"群体交互实验"涉及两个层面的随机化（个体到群体、群体到处理），且群体内个体之间的交互可能产生干扰效应。然而，现有的基于设计的推断理论尚未系统性地覆盖这类复杂实验设计，缺乏对其因果目标（causal estimand）和适当推断策略的统一刻画。

Method: 作者在稀疏抽样渐近框架下，对四种情景进行了系统分析：（1）群体固定、无干扰；（2）群体随机形成、无干扰；（3）群体固定、存在干扰；（4）群体随机形成、存在干扰。针对每种情景，作者刻画了设计所瞄准的因果估计量，并推导了适当的标准误估计量。核心技术贡献是一种新颖的"耦合策略"（coupling strategy），用于在复杂实验设计中推导常用估计量的渐近分布，从而建立聚类稳健推断（cluster-robust inference）和异方差稳健推断（heteroskedasticity-robust inference）的渐近有效性。

Result: （1）当存在干扰时，聚类稳健推断在群体层面保持一致且有效，能够对"边际化暴露效应"（marginalized exposure effects）提供有效推断，并考虑多种来源的依赖关系。（2）当不存在干扰且群体随机形成时，该设计退化为个体随机实验，个体层面的异方差稳健推断足以用于平均处理效应。（3）所提出的耦合策略为其他复杂实验中的基于设计的推断提供了通用工具。

Conclusion: 本文为群体交互实验提供了完整的基于设计的推断框架，明确了不同实验情景下适当的因果估计量与推断方法。研究表明，聚类稳健推断是处理群体交互实验中干扰效应的有效且通用的策略，而群体随机形成且无干扰时则可简化为标准个体随机化推断。提出的耦合策略具有更广泛的方法论价值。

Abstract: A common experimental research design is one in which individuals are randomly allocated into groups that then interact under different group-level treatment conditions. We develop design-based inference for such "group interaction" experiments, covering scenarios in which groups are either fixed or randomly formed and in which potential outcomes are either fixed relative to others' group assignments or subject to interference. For each scenario, we characterize the causal estimand that the design targets and the inferential strategy appropriate to it. Working in a sparse-sampling asymptotic regime, we show that cluster-robust inference remains consistent and accounts for dependencies from various sources when interference is present, delivering valid inference on marginalized exposure effects. When interference is absent and groups are formed randomly, the design reduces to an individually randomized experiment, and individual-level heteroskedasticity-robust inference suffices for the average treatment effect. Our results on the asymptotic distribution of commonly used estimators rely on a novel coupling strategy that may be useful for design-based inference in other complex experiments.

</details>


### [297] [Moment-Based Selection of Multiresponse Linear Mixed-Effects Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01971)
*Yifan Chen,Yuedong Wang,Guo Yu*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: MOMENT 是一种基于矩的阶段性混合效应选择与估计框架，利用二阶交叉矩恒等式来选择和估计随机效应协方差矩阵及固定效应系数。通过在对角线上引入稀疏性并结合正半定约束，随机效应选择问题转化为光滑约束凸优化问题，可用投影梯度下降高效求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在混合效应模型中，随机效应协方差矩阵的选择与估计是一个关键挑战，尤其当响应变量相关时。现有方法多依赖单独的单变量分析，忽略了响应间的相关性，导致效率低下。本文旨在提出一个统一框架，同时进行随机效应和固定效应的选择与估计，以提升多元纵向数据分析的准确性和可解释性。

Method: 提出 MOMENT 框架，核心包括：(1) 利用二阶交叉矩恒等式建立估计方程；(2) 通过对随机效应协方差矩阵的对角线施加稀疏性诱导，并在正半定约束下将问题转化为光滑约束凸优化；(3) 采用投影梯度下降算法进行高效求解；(4) 在联合 sub-Weibull 误差假设下建立有限样本理论保证。

Result: 理论上建立了随机效应选择一致性和固定效应选择一致性的有限样本保证。模拟研究表明，MOMENT 整体性能具有竞争力，且在响应变量相关时可显著优于单独的单变量分析。在血液透析数据集上的应用展示了该方法对多元纵向数据的可解释性和灵活性。

Conclusion: MOMENT 为混合效应模型中的协方差选择和系数估计提供了一个统一、高效且有理论保证的框架，特别适用于响应相关的多元纵向数据场景，兼具统计精度和计算可行性。

Abstract: We propose MOMENT (\textbf{MO}ment-Based \textbf{M}ixed-\textbf{E}ffects Selectio\textbf{N} and Es\textbf{T}imation), a stage-wise moment-based framework that exploits second-order cross-moment identities to select and estimate the random-effects covariance matrix and fixed-effects coefficients. By inducing sparsity through its diagonal under a positive semidefinite constraint, the random-effects selection problem reduces to a smooth constrained convex optimization problem that can be solved efficiently by projected gradient descent. We further establish finite-sample theoretical guarantees for the proposed procedure, including random-effects selection consistency and fixed-effects selection consistency under joint sub-Weibull errors. Simulation studies show that MOMENT performs competitively overall and can substantially outperform separate univariate analyses when responses are correlated. An application to the hemodialysis dataset demonstrates that the proposed method yields an interpretable and flexible approach for multivariate longitudinal data.

</details>


### [298] [Sequential Structure-Sensitive Residual Diagnostics for PDE Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02101)
*Ieva Kazlauskaite*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于e-processes的结构敏感序贯诊断方法，用于检测平滑反问题中的模型误设。该方法通过一组空间残差模式专家的财富更新，在残差范数仍低于传统阈值时即可提前发现结构化错误，并在三个反问题中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统残差范数诊断（如Morozov偏差准则）在平滑反问题中存在根本性缺陷：结构化模型误差在观测空间中可能被衰减，导致残差范数虽小但残差呈现相干模式，从而使有偏的拟合模型被错误接受，影响参数估计和预测的可靠性。需要一种对残差结构敏感的序贯检验方法。

Method: 基于e-processes框架，构建一组空间残差模式专家（专家库），每个专家对应一种预期的残差结构模式。序贯处理观测数据时，利用似然比更新各专家的财富（wealth），当聚合财富超过预设阈值时拒绝拟合模型。该方法对固定拟合模型提供随时有效的I类错误控制（anytime-valid type-I error control）。

Result: 在椭圆扩散、二维Stokes流和冰盖流（icepack）三个反问题中，Morozov偏差检验接受了产生实质性偏差的误设模型；批量结构敏感检验能用全数据集检测出这些失败；而e-process方法仅需部分观测即可更早检测到模型误设。拒绝后，专家财富可归因于字典中的特定残差模式，为探索性模型修正提供依据。

Conclusion: e-process序贯诊断方法能有效弥补传统残差范数检验在平滑反问题中的不足，实现更早、更灵敏的模型误设检测，并具有可解释性强、支持在线部署和提供修正方向等优势。

Abstract: Computational models in science and engineering are often assessed by checking whether the residual norm is consistent with the assumed noise level. This can be misleading in smoothing inverse problems: structured model errors may be attenuated in observation space, leaving residual magnitudes below practitioner discrepancy thresholds while coherent residual patterns remain. As a result, residual-norm diagnostics can accept fitted models that still give biased parameters, predictions, or quantities of interest. We propose a structure-sensitive sequential diagnostic based on e-processes. The method uses a portfolio of spatial residual-pattern experts, updates their likelihood-ratio wealth as observations are processed, and rejects the fitted model when the aggregate wealth crosses a prescribed threshold, giving anytime-valid type-I error control for a fixed fitted model. We compare the method with Morozov discrepancy checks, fixed-sample residual tests, and batch projection tests. Across three inverse problems (elliptic diffusion, two-dimensional Stokes flow, and a glaciological ice-stream inversion implemented in the community finite-element model icepack) we demonstrate how standard discrepancy checks accept misspecified fits that produce materially wrong quantities of interest. Structure-sensitive batch tests detect these failures using the full dataset, while the e-process detects them earlier from a fraction of the observations. After rejection, the expert wealth attributes the evidence to residual patterns in the chosen dictionary and provides a basis for exploratory model correction.

</details>


### [299] [Conformal Bayes for Two-Sided Censored Gaussian Regression under Label Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02173)
*Seungjin Choi*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了针对双侧截尾高斯模型下标签偏移的共形贝叶斯方法，通过后验预测倾斜与加权共形校准相结合，处理混合空间（边界原子+连续内部）的预测问题，并在合成实验中验证了其边际覆盖率和集合大小的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当响应变量被截尾时，标签偏移下的预测问题变得非标准。传统方法难以处理观测空间中同时包含边界原子（L和U处）和连续密度（L,U）的混合分布结构。

Method: 采用双侧Tobit高斯贝叶斯预测头配合拉普拉斯后验近似，构建倾斜预测分布（含左原子、内部、右原子三部分，具有三项闭合形式归一化器）。核心创新在于：潜在指数倾斜在截尾边界诱导出尾部平均的原子权重，而内部保持密度比形式，由此得到包含两个原子比和一个内部密度比的混合观测空间校准权重。该权重修正校准测度，预测倾斜则提供目标自适应的混合最高密度区域几何结构。

Result: 合成实验表明，加权倾斜共形贝叶斯恢复了边际覆盖率，且集合大小小于加权源得分校准方法；同时揭示了边际覆盖率与原子/内部观测分量行为之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效解决了截尾响应下的标签偏移预测问题，混合HDR预测集可灵活退化为纯原子集（强截尾场景），为混合空间的共形预测提供了新框架。

Abstract: Prediction under label shift becomes nonstandard when responses are censored. In a two-sided censored Gaussian model, latent values below $L$ and above $U$ are recorded at the boundary values, so the observed predictive distribution is mixed, with atoms at $L$ and $U$ and a continuous density on $(L,U)$. In this paper we develop conformal Bayes for this mixed-space setting by combining posterior predictive tilting with weighted conformal calibration. Under a two-sided Tobit Gaussian Bayesian prediction head with a Laplace posterior approximation, the tilted predictive distribution has left-atom, interior, and right-atom components, with a three-term closed-form normalizer. The resulting prediction set is a mixed highest density region that can combine boundary atoms with an interior interval and can reduce to atom-only sets under strong censoring. The main technical issue is that latent label shift does not directly give an ordinary density ratio on the observed censored scale. A latent exponential tilt induces tail-averaged atom weights at the censored boundaries, while the interior ratio remains density based. This yields a mixed observed-space calibration weight with two atom ratios and one interior density ratio. The weight corrects the calibration measure, while predictive tilting gives target-adapted mixed-HDR geometry. Synthetic experiments show that weighted tilted conformal Bayes restores marginal coverage with smaller sets than weighted source-score calibration, while revealing a trade-off between marginal coverage and component-wise behavior across atoms and interior observations.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [300] [PACE: A Neuro-Symbolic Framework for Plausible and Actionable Counterfactual Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01306)
*Pavel Iakovets,Liyanapathiranage Sudeepika Wajirakumari Samarathunga,Martin Thomas Horsch,Fadi Al Machot*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PACE是一个模块化的神经符号框架，通过将神经网络预测与基于ASP的符号推理相分离，在生成反事实解释时显式编码领域约束，从而产生既改变预测又符合现实可行性的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反事实解释方法虽然能生成改变预测的输入变动，但缺乏显式机制融入领域知识和干预约束，常导致不切实际或不可行的建议。神经符号AI为结合数据驱动预测与可解释的符号推理提供了新方向。

Method: 提出PACE框架，将系统分为两个组件：(1) 多层感知机(MLP)分类器进行预测；(2) 基于答案集编程(ASP)的符号推理层，在反事实生成过程中施加领域特定约束（如可修改的教育、职业、工时），同时保护不可变属性。该方法是模型无关的。

Result: 在Adult Income数据集上的案例研究表明，符号约束能在反事实有效性与合理性之间取得权衡，生成的反事实解释更好地满足领域特定的可行性要求。

Conclusion: 神经符号方法在可解释AI中具有前景，能够生成透明且可行性感知的反事实解释，适用于需要现实决策支持的领域。

Abstract: Counterfactual explanations explain machine learning predictions by identifying minimal input changes that would alter a model's decision. Although many existing methods successfully generate prediction-changing alternatives, they often produce unrealistic or infeasible recommendations due to a lack of explicit mechanisms for incorporating domain knowledge and intervention constraints. Neuro-symbolic AI offers a promising direction by combining data-driven predictive models with symbolic reasoning capable of representing human-understandable rules and feasible actions. This paper presents PACE, a modular neuro-symbolic framework for generating feasibility-aware counterfactual explanations. The framework separates prediction and reasoning into two components: a neural predictive model for classification and a symbolic reasoning layer that enforces domain-specific constraints during counterfactual generation. By explicitly modeling feasible interventions, the framework produces explanations consistent with domain knowledge while remaining interpretable and actionable. The approach is model-agnostic and adaptable to domains requiring realistic decision support. A case study is conducted on the Adult Income dataset, combining a multilayer perceptron classifier with Answer Set Programming (ASP) rules encoding feasible modifications to education, occupation, and working hours while preserving immutable attributes. Results highlight the trade-off between counterfactual validity and plausibility and show that symbolic constraints yield explanations that better satisfy domain-specific feasibility requirements, illustrating the potential of neuro-symbolic methods for transparent, feasibility-aware counterfactual explanation in explainable AI.

</details>


### [301] [Auto-FL-Research: Agentic Search for Federated Learning Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01366)
*Holger R. Roth,Ziyue Xu,Chester Chen,Daguang Xu,Peter Cnudde,Andrew Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Auto-FL-Research（AFR），一个受约束的编码智能体工作流，用于联邦学习算法配方的自动搜索。在FLamby和LEAF数据集上的实验表明，AFR能发现性能增益，但也揭示了种子敏感性、搜索选择偏差等问题，有助于区分真正的FL机制改进与调参效应及单次运行伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习研究中存在大量小而关键但难以穷举对比的算法选择（优化器变体、聚合规则、训练调度、归一化、正则化、模型架构等），手动探索成本高昂且因候选变更可能改变训练/评估路径而导致公平比较困难。

Method: 提出Auto-FL-Research（AFR）——一种受约束的编码智能体工作流。智能体可提议并实现候选训练算法（包括服务器聚合规则、客户端更新调度、本地目标函数及模型变体），任务配置文件则固定变异表面、计算预算、通信合约和最终模型评估。每次搜索活动记录候选分数、运行时间、编辑文件、产物及失败状态。

Result: 在五个医疗跨中心FLamby任务和五个固定LEAF数据集加LEAF合成任务的grouped-client配置上进行评估。五种子重复实验显示：四个FLamby任务和六个LEAF配置中的五个获得增益；同时暴露了种子敏感和搜索选择的失败案例。等预算对照表明部分增益源自FL配方变化，其他则来自固定表面的标量调参，或在重复/留出评估下失效。

Conclusion: AFR能有效自动搜索联邦学习算法配方，但混合结果表明需谨慎解读：智能体生成的候选可被区分为可复现的FL机制改进、固定表面调参效应和被选中的单次运行伪影，这一区分本身就是方法的重要贡献。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) research often depends on many small but consequential algorithmic choices: optimizer variants, server aggregation rules, local training schedules, normalization, regularization, and model architecture. These choices are expensive to explore manually and difficult to compare fairly when candidate changes can also alter the FL training or evaluation path. In this work, we present Auto-FL-Research (AFR), a constrained coding-agent workflow for FL algorithmic recipe search. Agents may propose and implement candidate training algorithms, including server aggregation rules, client update schedules, local objectives, and registered model variants, while task profiles fix the mutation surface, compute budget, communication contract, and final model evaluation. Each campaign records candidate scores, runtime, edited files, artifacts, and failure status. We evaluate AFR on five healthcare cross-silo FLamby tasks and on grouped-client profiles for the five fixed LEAF datasets plus the LEAF synthetic task. Five-seed repeat evaluations support gains on four FLamby tasks and five of six LEAF profiles, while also exposing seed-sensitive and search-selected failure cases. Same-budget controls show that several gains correspond to FL-recipe changes, whereas other improvements are recovered by fixed-surface scalar controls or fail under repeat or held-out evaluation. These mixed outcomes are part of the contribution: they show how agent-generated candidates can be separated into repeated FL mechanisms, fixed-surface tuning effects, and selected single-run artifacts.

</details>


### [302] [The Wiola Architecture for Efficient Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01394)
*Aryuemaan Kumar Chowdhury,Afreen Shaik,Yaparla Bhargavi,Brahma Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Wiola 是一个全新的小型语言模型架构，从零设计，与现有模型家族无结构关联。它包含五个独立创新组件：螺旋旋转位置编码(SRPE)、门控跨层注意力(GCLA)、自适应令牌合并(ATM)、双流前馈网络(DSFF)和改进的 RMSNorm。该模型发布了四种参数规模，同时兼容 HuggingFace 生态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前主流语言模型架构（GPT、LLaMA、Mistral 等）存在结构同质化问题，作者旨在从第一性原理出发，设计一个完全原创的、不继承任何现有模型结构血脉的小型语言模型，同时通过多项架构创新解决位置编码表达能力不足、跨层信息流动不畅、注意力复杂度高以及表示坍缩等问题。

Method: 提出 Wiola 架构，包含五项独立创新：(1) SRPE：在三维螺旋流形上嵌入位置信息，融合绝对、相对和层次位置信号；(2) GCLA：每层解码器通过软交叉注意力访问前两层的压缩摘要；(3) ATM：在网络中间层动态合并语义冗余的相邻令牌以降低注意力复杂度；(4) DSFF：用两个并行的前馈流替代传统 MLP，通过可学习的逐维度门控融合；(5) WiolaRMSNorm：在 RMSNorm 基础上引入可学习的逐维度偏移向量防止表示坍缩。同时提供完整数学推导、架构图、复杂度分析和系统性对比实验。

Result: 发布了四种参数规模的 Wiola 模型（120M、360M、700M、1.5B），全部 22 项架构单元测试通过，完全兼容 HuggingFace Transformers 生态。与 GPT-2、LLaMA-2、Mistral 进行了系统性对比。

Conclusion: Wiola 证明了从第一性原理出发、不依赖现有架构范式设计全新语言模型结构的可行性，其五项独立创新组件各自针对现有架构的特定缺陷提供了解决方案，为语言模型架构设计的多样性探索开辟了新方向。

Abstract: We present Wiola, a fully original Small Language Model (SLM) architecture built from first principles, sharing no structural lineage with any existing model family including GPT, LLaMA, Mistral, or Falcon. Wiola introduces five independently novel components: (i) Spiral Rotary Positional Encoding (SRPE), which embeds token positions on a three-dimensional helical manifold combining absolute, relative, and hierarchical positional signals; (ii) Gated Cross-Layer Attention (GCLA), providing each decoder layer with soft cross-attention access to compressed summaries of two preceding layers for inter-layer coherence; (iii) Adaptive Token Merging (ATM), which dynamically merges se mantically redundant adjacent tokens in middle network layers to reduce attention complexity without information loss; (iv) Dual Stream Feed-Forward (DSFF), replacing the conventional MLP with two parallel streams fused by a learned per-dimension gate; and (v) WiolaRMSNorm, a modified normalisation introducing a per-dimension learned offset vector that prevents representation collapse. We provide complete mathematical derivations, architectural block diagrams, complexity analyses, and systematic comparisons against GPT-2, LLaMA-2, and Mistral. Wiola is released in four sizes (120M, 360M, 700M, and 1.5B parameters) and is fully compatible with the HuggingFace Transformers ecosystem, with all 22 architectural unit tests passing.

</details>


### [303] [Agent4cs: A Multi-agent System for Code Summarization in Large Hierarchical Codebases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01425)
*Yongjian Tang,Ezgi Sarikayak,Doruk Tuncel,Jie M. Zhang,Thomas Runkler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Agent4cs多智能体框架，采用自底向上方式对大型代码库进行摘要，通过摘要智能体、关键词提取智能体和质量保证智能体协同工作，在语义一致性和关键词覆盖率上显著优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码摘要方法通常将源代码视为扁平文本，未能充分利用代码仓库中丰富的依赖关系和层次结构信息，且多依赖单一语言模型，难以应对混淆结构和不完整文档带来的挑战。

Method: 提出Agent4cs多智能体框架，采用自底向上的摘要策略：摘要智能体负责生成高质量摘要，关键词提取智能体主动从子文件夹中识别关键信息，质量保证智能体迭代优化输出的可读性、连贯性和完整性。

Result: 在7个前沿模型上评估，相比两种结构化提示基线方法，Agent4cs在所有文件夹层级上的语义一致性平均提升8%；在真实数据集上，归一化关键词覆盖率达到高达38%的提升。

Conclusion: Agent4cs通过多智能体分工协作与自底向上的层次化摘要策略，有效挖掘代码仓库的结构化信息，显著提升了大型代码库的摘要质量。

Abstract: Understanding large, complex codebases, especially those with obfuscated structures and incomplete documentation, remains a significant challenge. Existing code summarization solutions often rely on a single language model or coding assistant like Claude Code, and treat source code as flat text, underutilizing the rich interdependencies and hierarchical information within a repository. To address these shortcomings, we propose Agent4cs - a multi-agent framework that summarizes large codebases in a bottom-up fashion, where a summarization agent focuses on producing robust summaries; a keyword-extraction agent proactively identifies critical information from subfolders; and a quality-assurance agent iteratively refines the outputs for readability, coherence, and completeness. Evaluated on 7 frontier models, Agent4cs improves semantic consistency across all folder levels by average 8% compared to two structured prompting baselines with code segments. Furthermore, extensive evaluation on real-world datasets demonstrates up to 38% gains in normalized keyword coverage rate over the same baselines.

</details>


### [304] [When Should Service Agents Reconsider? Difficulty-Routed Control in Customer-Service Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01426)
*Qian Chen,Chengyuan Liu,Xin Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种难度路由的服务控制架构，通过轻量级路由器将常规客服会话保持在低成本基线路径，而将存在操作耦合的会话升级到增强工作流中，该工作流采用冲突感知沟通和写触发式重新审议机制，在关键后端写入前集中进行审慎核查，从而在不影响常规服务效率的前提下提升操作可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主客服智能体正从对话交互转向操作执行（如退款、取消、换货等），但存在服务控制难题：如何在保持常规服务快速低摩擦的同时，防止客户指令、策略约束、企业记录和后端写入交互作用下的操作错误。

Method: 提出难度路由服务控制架构：1）轻量级路由器识别是否存在操作耦合，将常规会话保持在低成本基线路径，将问题会话路由至升级工作流；2）升级路径采用冲突感知通信和写触发式重新审议（write-triggered reconsideration），在后端写入前集中进行审慎核查和安全防护。

Result: 在τ²-bench的零售和航空任务上进行评估。零售场景中，该方法在存在操作冲突的服务请求上持续提升可靠性；路由证据表明更强的控制被定向到冲突请求而非广泛施加于常规请求；对话和工具使用分析显示增益并非来自无差别的交互扩展，而是来自证据收集、写入分离和写入前重新审议。航空场景结果将相同的服务控制逻辑扩展到预订操作。

Conclusion: 难度路由架构能有效在保持常规服务效率的同时提升操作可靠性，通过将审慎控制集中于可能存在操作冲突的关键写入环节，而非对所有会话一视同仁地施加额外控制。

Abstract: Autonomous customer-service agents are shifting from conversational interfaces toward operational execution roles: they retrieve firm records, apply service policies, and execute backend writes such as refunds, cancellations, exchanges, order modifications, and reservation changes. This shift creates a service-control problem: firms must keep routine service fast and low-friction while preventing operational errors on requests where customer instructions, policy constraints, firm records, and backend writes interact. We propose a difficulty-routed service-control architecture that asks when service agents should reconsider before acting. A lightweight router keeps routine sessions on a low-cost baseline path and routes operationally coupled sessions to an escalated workflow. The escalated path uses conflict-aware communication and write-triggered reconsideration to concentrate deliberation and safeguards before consequential backend writes, rather than applying additional control uniformly across all service sessions. We evaluate the architecture on human-verified retail and airline tasks from $τ^{2}$-bench. In retail, the method improves reliability consistently on service requests with operational conflict. Routing evidence shows that stronger control is directed toward conflicted requests rather than broadly applied to routine ones. Dialogue and tool-use profiles suggest that gains do not come from indiscriminate interaction expansion or broader tool chains; instead, added turns and tool calls support evidence gathering, write separation, and pre-write reconsideration. Case-level evidence shows that the escalated workflow preserves fallback plans, binds retrieved records to the correct action, sequences writes, and decomposes multi-entity requests. Airline results extend the same service-control logic to reservation operations.

</details>


### [305] [CreativityNeuro: Steering Language Model Weights to Improve Divergent Thinking and Reduce Mode Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01433)
*Samuel Schapiro,Core Francisco Park,Felix Sosa,Lav R. Varshney*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CreativityNeuro是一种无需数据的对比权重引导方法，通过修改LLM权重空间来增强发散性思维，在多项创造力测试中显著提升原创性和惊喜度，同时有效减少模式坍塌，且无需额外训练数据或微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型在回答开放式问题时倾向于生成相似回复，即"人工蜂巢思维效应"（artificial hivemind effect），这严重限制了模型在创造性任务中的发散思维能力。现有方法多依赖行为数据或梯度微调，缺乏一种简洁、无需数据、可泛化的解决方案。

Method: 提出CreativityNeuro，一种基于对比权重引导（contrastive weight steering）的无数据方法。该方法直接在权重空间中对LLM进行干预，通过对比引导机制调整模型参数，以增强输出的发散性和多样性，区别于激活引导（activation steering）仅在激活空间操作。

Result: 在发散联想任务（DAT）上，CreativityNeuro将性能提升高达14个人类百分位点；在包含720人的大规模人工评估中（替代用途测试AUT和Task Task），该方法在原创性、惊喜度和创造力三个维度上均取得显著提升，并成功迁移到更长文本、更开放的任务中。此外，激活引导虽在DAT上表现相当，但无法迁移至AUT和Task Task，而权重空间引导则展现了更强的跨任务泛化能力。三种任务上均观察到模式坍塌指标的显著降低。

Conclusion: CreativityNeuro无需行为数据、重新训练或基于梯度的微调，即可有效提升LLM的发散性思维能力并减少模式坍塌，为增强模型在创造性领域的表现提供了一条简洁高效的路径。

Abstract: Divergent thinking is a crucial aspect of creativity, yet large language models (LLMs) tend to consistently generate similar responses to open-ended questions, in what has been termed the artificial hivemind effect. Here, we introduce CreativityNeuro, a data-free method for enhancing divergent thinking in LLMs via contrastive weight steering. We evaluate our method across multiple creativity assessments and report several main findings. On the Divergent Association Task (DAT), a vocabulary-space creativity test, CreativityNeuro improves performance by up to 14 human percentile points. Next, in a large-scale human evaluation (N=720) on the Alternative Uses Test (AUT) and the Task Task, CreativityNeuro achieves significant improvements in originality, surprise, and creativity, transferring to longer-form and more open-ended tasks. Importantly, we find that across all three tasks, CreativityNeuro demonstrably reduces measures of mode collapse. Moreover, activation steering achieves comparable performance to CreativityNeuro on the DAT, but it does not transfer to the AUT and Task Task, demonstrating the effectiveness of weight-space steering in generalizing to unseen tasks. In conclusion, CreativityNeuro improves divergent thinking and reduces mode collapse without requiring behavioral data, re-training, or gradient-based fine-tuning, providing a straightforward way to enhance LLM performance in creative domains.

</details>


### [306] [Discrete Diffusion Language Models for Interactive Radiology Report Drafting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01436)
*Max Van Puyvelde,Halil Ibrahim Gulluk,Wim Van Criekinge,Olivier Gevaert*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文将扩散语言模型DiffusionGemma-26B适配到医疗视觉问答任务，在相同LoRA微调方案下与同规模自回归模型Gemma-4-26B对比，扩散模型在各项指标上匹配或超越自回归模型，解码速度快3.5-4.4倍，且具备自回归模型缺乏的任意顺序文本填充能力，特别适合放射报告等真实临床场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前医疗基础模型几乎全部采用自回归架构，而扩散语言模型在通用文本生成中已展现出竞争力。作者旨在验证扩散语言模型在医疗领域能否匹敌甚至超越自回归模型，并探索扩散模型特有的双向去噪机制带来的临床实用优势（如任意顺序文本填充）。

Method: 采用混合专家（MoE）扩散语言模型DiffusionGemma-26B，在医疗视觉问答数据集上使用LoRA高效微调，与同等规模的自回归模型Gemma-4-26B进行对比评估；评分由对冗长输出鲁棒的LLM评判器完成；同时验证扩散模型特有的任意顺序填充能力。

Result: 扩散模型在所有医疗VQA数据集上匹配或超越自回归模型；微调后模型（3.8B活跃参数）性能可与前沿视觉语言模型竞争；解码速度提升3.5-4.4倍；扩散模型展现出独有的任意顺序填充能力：放射科医生可修正报告片段，模型自动填充中间内容，自回归模型在此任务上表现欠佳。

Conclusion: 扩散语言模型在医疗领域不仅是自回归模型的有力替代方案，其在速度与灵活的文本填充能力上的优势使其在放射报告生成等真实临床场景中具有独特价值，有望推动医疗基础模型从单一自回归范式向扩散范式拓展。

Abstract: Diffusion language models, which generate text by denoising a token canvas bidirectionally instead of emitting tokens left to right, have become competitive with autoregressive (AR) generation. Medical foundation models, however, remain almost entirely autoregressive. We adapt a mixture-of-experts diffusion language model, DiffusionGemma-26B, and benchmark it against its same-size AR sibling Gemma-4-26B under an identical LoRA recipe on medical visual question answering datasets, scored by a verbosity-robust LLM judge. Diffusion matches or exceeds AR on all of them, and the finetuned model (3.8B active) is competitive with frontier vision-language models; its decoding is also 3.5-4.4x faster. Beyond this parity, the diffusion model offers a drafting capability AR lacks: any-order infill. Because the canvas is denoised bidirectionally, a radiologist can fix report fragments and have the model fill the text between them, an operation inherent to diffusion but not to autoregression, which is subpar at it. This suits real reports, which are often terse or inconsistent across clinicians and institutions.

</details>


### [307] [Beyond Next-Token Prediction: An RLVR Proof of Concept for Tool-Use Agents on Atlassian Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01465)
*Karthikeya Aditya Vissa,Sankalp Mane,Ananya Mantravadi,Harshit Rajgarhia,Abhishek Mukherji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探索利用可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）来弥合大语言模型在预测下一个token与在特定企业API内执行操作之间的目标错位。通过在模拟Jira和Confluence API的合成环境中使用GRPO训练，将小型模型（1.7B/4B）在四个非退化场景中的平均奖励从0.35–0.92提升至0.95–1.00。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型被训练为预测下一个token，而非在特定API内执行操作。在细分企业SaaS工作流中，这种目标错位表现为静默失败：遗漏必填字段、幻觉工具调用、或在单次读取后过早停止。现有方法依赖真实API、学习型评判器或人工标注，成本高昂且难以规模化。作者希望验证：直接在目标环境中应用RLVR，能否在不引入任何外部监督信号的情况下弥合这一差距。

Method: 构建五个模拟Jira REST v3与Confluence v2 API的合成环境（具有模式保真度）；奖励完全基于工具调用轨迹（tool-call trace）进行计算，无需真实API调用、无需学习型评判器、无需人工标注。使用相同的检查器（checker）对模型进行评分并驱动GRPO训练。在Qwen3-1.7B和Qwen3.5-4B上进行实验，对比提示工程基线（prompted baseline）与RL训练后策略的奖励得分。

Result: 在四个奖励非退化的场景中，RL训练策略将4B基线模型的平均奖励从0.35–0.92提升至0.95–1.00。其中最大单次增益出现在Confluence页面创建场景，从0.35跃升至1.00。五个场景中有一个（ticket-transition）基线4B模型已使奖励饱和，无法体现训练增益。

Conclusion: 该工作是面向细分企业API的“结果优化小型模型”的初步探索。RLVR在该设置中有效缩小了目标错位。但存在两个关键局限：(1) 手工构建可验证奖励无法在少数端点之外规模化；(2) 部分场景奖励形状已饱和，限制了RL的增益空间。

Abstract: Large language models are trained to predict the next token, not to act inside a specific API. In niche enterprise SaaS workflows -- where success means hitting the right endpoint with the right nested arguments in the right order -- this objective mismatch shows up as silent failures: dropped required fields, hallucinated tools, or early stops after a single read. We ask whether Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR), applied directly in the target environment, closes the gap. As a proof of concept we build a suite of five synthetic environments emulating the Jira REST v3 and Confluence v2 APIs at schema fidelity; rewards are computed entirely from the tool-call trace, with no live API, no learned judge, and no human label in the loop. Scoring prompted Qwen3-1.7B and Qwen3.5-4B on the same checkers that drive GRPO training, we find that on the four scenarios whose rewards are non-degenerate the RL-trained policy lifts average reward from a 4B-baseline range of 0.35--0.92 to 0.95--1.00, with the largest single gain on Confluence page creation ($0.35 \rightarrow 1.00$). We position this as a preliminary step toward outcome-optimised small models for niche enterprise APIs, and foreground two limitations a workshop reader should weigh: hand-crafting verifiable rewards does not scale beyond the handful of endpoints reported here, and one of our five scenarios (ticket-transition) has a saturating reward shape that the prompted 4B already maxes out.

</details>


### [308] [World Feedback for Clinical Agents: Diagnosing RL in FHIR Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01470)
*Ananya Mantravadi,Harshit Rajgarhia,Prasanna Desikan,Abhishek Mukherji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 分析了在临床协议执行任务上应用强化学习(RL)的可行性，发现MedAgentBench基准存在严重的数据质量缺陷，并通过构建新基准和训练实验，揭示了RL在临床编码任务中面临的能力天花板和格式知识壁垒，最终提出决策/格式知识/查找的三分类法来预测RL可学习性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床协议执行任务（如检查检验值、应用阈值、生成FHIR订单）天然适合RL训练，因为临床专家可将决策逻辑编码为验证器，无需逐条标注即可批量评分。然而RL的有效应用需要可靠反馈渠道和足够的基础能力，现有基准MedAgentBench是否满足这些条件尚待审计。

Method: 1）对MedAgentBench v1/v2进行审计，发现41.7%的静默完成上限（即不做任何操作即可通过的任务比例），构建了修复后的MedAgentBench-v3（508个任务，上限降至8.9%）；2）使用Qwen3-8B进行RL训练，系统分析各任务类型的基线表现；3）提出三分类框架：决策类、格式知识类、查找类，用于预测RL可学习性。

Result: 纯RL训练的pass@1仅达18.2%，而基于规则的SFT达到34.1%，15.9个百分点的差距完全归因于两个结构性障碍：（1）能力天花板：20种任务类型中有10种基线表现为0%，无梯度信号；（2）格式知识壁垒：3种任务类型需要精确的临床编码，单纯探索无法发现。三分类法可有效预测各任务类型的RL可学习性。

Conclusion: RL在临床协议执行任务上的成功需对症下药：通过SFT注入不可探索的格式知识（如精确临床编码），再通过RL学习条件决策逻辑。决策/格式知识/查找的三分类法为合理分配SFT与RL提供了指导框架。

Abstract: Clinical protocol-execution tasks -- checking a lab value, applying a threshold, placing a correctly structured FHIR order -- are natural candidates for RL from world feedback: once clinical SMEs encode decision logic into a verifier, that verifier grades unlimited rollouts without per-episode annotation. But applying RL requires a sound feedback channel and sufficient base capability. We audit MedAgentBench v1/v2, find a 41.7\% silent-finish ceiling that makes inaction the RL dominant strategy, and construct \textbf{MedAgentBench-v3 (MAB-v3)} (508 tasks, 8.9\% ceiling). Training Qwen3-8B exposes two structural barriers: a \emph{capability ceiling} (10/20 task types have 0\% base performance, zero gradient) and a \emph{format-knowledge barrier} (3/20 types require exact clinical codes undiscoverable by exploration). Pure RL reaches 18.2\% pass@1 vs.\ 34.1\% for rule-based SFT; the 15.9~pp gap is attributable entirely to these barriers. A decision/format-knowledge/lookup taxonomy predicts RL learnability and prescribes the fix: SFT to inject codes, RL to learn conditionals.

</details>


### [309] [Procedural Memory Distillation: Online Reflection for Self-Improving Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01480)
*Ye Liu,Srijan Bansal,Bo Pang,Yang Li,Zeyu Leo Liu,Yifei Ming,Zixuan Ke,Shafiq Joty,Semih Yavuz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出程序性记忆蒸馏(PMD)，将跨episode的验证信号转化为三层可复用记忆，通过记忆与策略的协同演化，显著提升RLVR/SDPO在科学知识和代码基准上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR及SDPO等方法仅在episode级别利用验证信号更新策略，忽略了rollout中更丰富的程序性信息，以及跨episode反复出现的策略模式、失败模式和验证规律，这些跨episode信号无法被episode-local更新所捕获。

Method: 提出PMD框架，在三个抽象层级组织记忆：(1)原始轨迹、(2)自我反思的策略与教训、(3)跨问题重复出现的高层行为模式，均从模型自身轨迹中在线提取。核心设计原则是协同演化——策略生成rollout更新记忆，记忆反过来塑造监督信号来更新策略。通过记忆条件化的自教师(memory-conditioned self-teacher)对学生的rollout进行监督，使学生逐步将程序性知识内化到参数中，推理时无需记忆模块。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B和OLMo3-Instruct-7B上，PMD相比SDPO在SCIKNOWEVAL上提升3.8-5.5%，在LIVECODEBENCH上提升7.9-13.6%。消融实验表明，冻结记忆或策略均导致SCIKNOWEVAL各领域性能下降超过10%，验证了协同演化的关键作用。

Conclusion: PMD通过将跨episode信号组织为程序性记忆并与策略协同演化，实现了程序性知识向模型参数的有效蒸馏，为RLVR训练提供了新的记忆增强范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), along with recent selfdistillation variants such as SDPO, evaluates each rollout against a verifier and updates the policy from that episode-level signal. However, the richer procedural information in the rollout is rarely retained or reused. Across episodes and epochs, the model repeatedly encounters related problems under a changing policy, producing cross-episode signals that episode-local updates cannot capture: which strategies consistently pass verification, which failure modes persist, which patterns recur. We propose Procedural Memory Distillation (PMD), which converts these crossepisode signals into reusable procedural memory and distills it into the policy's weights during training. This memory functions as a training scaffold, absorbed into the policy itself, yielding a memory-free model at inference. PMD organizes the memory at three levels of abstraction: raw trajectories, self-reflected strategies and lessons, and higher-level behavioral patterns that recur across problems, all extracted online from the model's own trajectories. A memory-conditioned self-teacher draws on the accumulated experience to supervise the student on its own rollouts, enabling student to progressively internalize procedural knowledge within its parameters. The central design principle is co-evolution: the policy generates rollouts that update the memory, and memory shapes the supervision that updates the policy. Empirically, across Qwen3-8B and OLMo3-Instruct-7B, PMD improves over SDPO by 3.8-5.5% on SCIKNOWEVAL and 7.9-13.6% on LIVECODEBENCH. Co-evolution powers these gains: freezing either the memory or the policy trails PMD by more than 10% across SCIKNOWEVAL domains.

</details>


### [310] [The Agentic Garden of Forking Paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01507)
*Jiacheng Miao,Jonathan K Pritchard,James Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示AI智能体能够复现人类研究者因分析路径选择不同而产生的结论分歧，并提出Agentic Bootstrap方法，通过估计m值（多宇宙值）来评估单一分析在合理分析分布中的位置，从而将分析路径的选择性探索转化为科学可信度的评估标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实证研究中，同一数据因分析选择不同可得出截然相反的结论，但这些"分叉路径"通常是隐性的、难以观测的。研究者希望揭示这一问题的系统性特征，并找到方法使其显性化、可量化。

Method: 1）赋予AI智能体不同"人设"（personas），令其在四个高风险领域对同一数据与问题进行分析，观察结论分歧；2）在42个人类团队分析过的移民数据集上，用AI智能体重现人类意识形态差异（复现率达72%）；3）提出m值（multiverse value），即某一分析路径产生至少同等极端结论的概率；4）提出Agentic Bootstrap方法，利用AI智能体采样合理的分析路径，估计m值分布。

Result: 1）AI智能体因不同人设产生了系统性对立的结论，且与预设信念一致；2）AI报告通过独立AI评审的比例为86%，通过多数人类专家评审的比例为78%，说明单篇报告难以识别问题；3）在人类移民研究中，13.5%的已报告分析落入最极端的5%分析空间（m<0.05）。

Conclusion: 核心挑战往往不在于分析本身的缺陷，而在于从大量方法论上可辩护的分析中进行选择性探索和报告。AI智能体可能加剧这一问题。科学证据应通过其在合理分析分布中的位置来评估，而不仅仅是依据单一报告的分析。Agentic Bootstrap使这一分布可观测，并将其转化为科学可信度的评判标准。

Abstract: Empirical research rarely admits a unique analysis. Different analytical choices can lead to different conclusions from the same data, yet these hidden forking paths are difficult to observe. We show that AI agents capture much of the analytical variation among human researchers while making these paths explicit. Across four high-stakes domains, assigning different personas is sufficient for AI agents to report divergent, often opposing, conclusions from the same data and question, with findings systematically aligned with those beliefs. In a study in which 42 human research teams analyzed the same immigration dataset, AI agents reproduced 72% of the human ideological gap in reported effect estimates. Despite reaching opposing conclusions, it is difficult to identify clear issues in each analysis based on the final AI reports: 86% passed independent AI review and 78% passed majority human expert review. These findings suggest that the central challenge is often not flawed analyses, but selective exploration and reporting from a large space of methodologically defensible analyses. AI agents may amplify this longstanding problem by making such exploration inexpensive and scalable. To address this, we introduce the m-value (multiverse value), the probability that an analysis path would produce a claim at least as extreme as the reported one. We further introduce Agentic Bootstrap, which estimates the m-value by using AI agents to sample plausible analysis paths. Applied to the human immigration study, 13.5% of reported human analyses fell in the most extreme 5% of the analysis space (m<0.05). Scientific evidence should therefore be evaluated not only by a single reported analysis but also by its position within the distribution of analyses that could reasonably have been reported. Agentic Bootstrap makes this distribution observable and turns it into a criterion for scientific credibility.

</details>


### [311] [Janus: a Playground for User-Involved Agentic Permission Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01510)
*Natalie Grace Brigham,Eugene Bagdasarian,Tadayoshi Kohno,Franziska Roesner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Janus 是一个用于设计和评估用户参与的智能体权限管理系统的平台，包含模块化智能体核心和自动化评估框架。研究通过六种权限助手设计、三个场景和三类合成响应者，证明用户输入对隐私安全至关重要，AI 增强决策可降低认知负担，且需要考虑权限疲劳等现实用户行为。没有单一设计在所有场景下最优，呼吁采用上下文感知的权限助手部署策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主执行工具调用的 AI 智能体带来了权限管理的紧迫问题：用户应扮演什么角色、能扮演什么角色。尽管已有多种方案被提出，但用户在智能体权限管理中的角色仍然探索不足。本文旨在系统性地填补这一空白。

Method: 提出 Janus 系统，包含两部分：(1) Janus-Core，一个模块化智能体系统，支持多样化权限管理设计；(2) Janus-Harness，一个自动化评估框架。基于识别用户参与关键设计轴的概念模型，在设计空间中实现了六种权限助手，并在三种场景和三种合成响应者下进行评估。

Result: 用户输入对增强隐私和安全至关重要；AI 对用户决策的增强可有效降低认知负担；系统设计必须考虑权限疲劳等现实用户行为；没有单一设计在所有场景下均表现最优，需要上下文敏感的策略。

Conclusion: 有效的智能体权限管理需要用户参与与 AI 辅助之间的精心平衡。应针对不同上下文采用有原则的、上下文感知的权限助手部署方法。Janus 平台已公开发布，以支持该方向的进一步研究。

Abstract: AI agents that autonomously execute tool calls on a user's behalf raise pressing questions about permission management: what role could users play, and what role should they play? Despite many proposed approaches, the user's role in agentic permission management remains under explored. We introduce Janus, a playground system for implementing and evaluating user-involved agentic permission management designs. Janus consists of two components: Janus-Core, a modular agentic system supporting a diverse spectrum of permission management designs, and Janus-Harness, an automated evaluation framework. Grounded in a conceptual model that identifies key design axes for user involvement, we implement six permission assistants spanning the design space and evaluate them across three scenarios and three synthetic responders. We demonstrate that user input is critical and can significantly strengthen privacy and security, that AI augmentation of user decisions can help reduce cognitive load, and that realistic user behavior including permission fatigue must be accounted for in system design. No single design performs optimally across all contexts, motivating a more principled and context-sensitive approach to deploying permission assistants in agentic systems. Janus is publicly available to support future investigation into this dimension of agentic system design.

</details>


### [312] [Revisiting Chain-of-Thought Reasoning under Limited Supervision: Semi-supervised Chain-of-Thought Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01511)
*Hongyang He,Jiuming Liu,Victor Sanchez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Semi-CoT 框架，利用无标注问题生成多条伪推理链，通过语义熵筛选高质量伪 CoT 作为半监督信号，初步实验显示熵门控可选出高精度伪标签，但下游增益有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 CoT 方法仅将推理链作为推理时的提示，生成的大量推理轨迹未被复用为半监督学习信号。作者希望探索将 CoT 从推理时优化拓展到半监督伪监督学习，利用无标注数据提升模型推理能力。

Method: 提出 Semi-supervised Chain-of-Thought Learning（Semi-CoT）框架：对每个无标注问题采样多条伪 CoT，估计答案级语义熵（semantic entropy），通过熵门控筛选低熵（高确定性）推理链作为可靠伪 CoT 示范，用于学生模型训练。

Result: 在 AQuA、SVAMP、GSM8K、MultiArith 上实验：熵门控选出的伪答案精度达 91.36%~100%；SVAMP 和 GSM8K 获得小幅提升，AQuA 出现负迁移，MultiArith 达到性能天花板。

Conclusion: 无标注问题可提供可靠伪推理信号，但有效利用仍需更强的示范选择策略或更好的学生训练方法。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning has emerged as an effective approach for activating latent reasoning capabilities in large language models. However, most existing CoT methods use reasoning chains mainly as inference-time prompts, while the generated reasoning traces are rarely reused as semi-supervised learning signals. In this report, we define \textbf{Semi-supervised Chain-of-Thought Learning} and propose \textbf{Semi-CoT}, a simple framework that uses unlabeled questions to construct pseudo reasoning supervision. Semi-CoT samples multiple pseudo-CoTs for each unlabeled question, estimates answer-level semantic entropy, and selects low-entropy reasoning chains as reliable pseudo-CoT demonstrations. This extends the self-training view of CoT from inference-time refinement to semi-supervised pseudo-supervision. Pilot experiments on AQuA, SVAMP, GSM8K, and MultiArith show that the entropy gate selects high-precision pseudo-CoTs, with pseudo-answer precision ranging from $91.36\%$ to $100\%$. Semi-CoT also gives small gains on SVAMP and GSM8K, while AQuA shows negative transfer and MultiArith reaches a ceiling. These results suggest that unlabeled questions can provide reliable pseudo reasoning signals, but their effective use still requires stronger demonstration selection or student training.

</details>


### [313] [OPINE-World: Programmatic World Modeling with Ontology-error-Prioritized Interactive Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01531)
*David Courtis,Wenhao Li,Scott Sanner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OPINE-World 是一个在线学习物体中心化程序化世界模型的LLM智能体。它通过两个协作智能体（一个在环境中行动，一个用代码合成模型）在假设-验证循环中工作，并利用本体论误差（ontology error）这一贝叶斯指标来引导探索。在ARC-AGI-3基准测试中，25个游戏中解决了20个，无需逐游戏训练，动作效率得分达78.4（对比人类基线）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度网络学习的世界模型数据需求大且分布外迁移差；LLM程序合成（CEGIS）虽然数据高效且可复用，但局限于已知物体词汇的结构化状态世界，难以扩展到需要灵活推断物体结构的像素渲染环境。本文旨在弥补这一差距，在像素环境中实现在线、数据高效的程序化世界模型学习。

Method: OPINE-World 包含两个协作的LLM智能体：一个"行动者"在环境中执行动作并收集经验；另一个"合成者"通过代码合成程序化世界模型，并利用重放验证（replay verification）和基于模型的规划进行迭代精化。探索策略由"本体论误差"（ontology error）驱动，这是一个贝叶斯度量，用于评估当前物体类型假设对观测数据的解释充分性，从而引导智能体优先探索模型不确定的区域。

Result: 在ARC-AGI-3基准的25个游戏中，OPINE-World 在无逐游戏训练的条件下解决了20个，动作效率得分达到78.4（相对于人类基线），证明了其在未知物体词汇、目标和动作语义条件下的高效技能习得能力。

Conclusion: OPINE-World 成功展示了LLM智能体可以在像素渲染环境中，通过在线交互学习灵活、可复用的程序化世界模型。本体论误差作为探索引导信号有效提升了数据效率。该方法在样本效率、泛化性和可解释性方面对比纯神经网络方法具有显著优势。

Abstract: Learning how an environment behaves from interaction is central to building agents that adapt to unfamiliar tasks. World models learned with deep networks are flexible but data-hungry and transfer poorly beyond their training distribution. Program-synthesized world models, written as source code by LLMs and refined through counterexample-guided inductive synthesis (CEGIS), are instead data-efficient and reusable, yet they have been demonstrated mainly on structured-state worlds with a given object vocabulary, and a single program search does not scale to pixel-rendered environments whose object structure must be hypothesized flexibly. We introduce OPINE-World, an LLM agent that learns an object-centric programmatic world model online from interaction. OPINE-World couples two cooperating agents in a loop of hypothesis and test, one acting in the environment and one synthesizing the model in code with replay verification and model-based planning, and it steers exploration with a Bayesian measure of object-type adequacy we call ontology error. We evaluate OPINE-World on ARC-AGI-3, a benchmark for skill-acquisition efficiency in which the object vocabulary, the goal, and the action semantics are withheld. OPINE-World solves 20 of 25 games without per-game training and reaches an action-efficiency score of 78.4 against the human baseline.

</details>


### [314] [Scaling Trends for Lie Detector Oversight in Preference Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01567)
*Oskar J. Hollinsworth,Ann-Kathrin Dombrowski,Sam Adam-Day,Adam Gleave,Chris Cundy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究将SOLiD（通过谎言检测器实现可扩展监督）方法扩展到更大模型和更多样化的偏好学习场景，发现其具有有利的缩放特性，但存在对分布偏移敏感的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM中的欺骗行为监控和预防成本高昂，现有的SOLiD方法虽能通过谎言检测器筛选需人工审查的响应，但缺乏在更大模型和真实偏好学习场景下的验证。

Method: 将SOLiD扩展到更大参数规模的模型（1B至405B），并在更多样化和现实的偏好学习设置中进行评估，测试在移除人工标注者的情况下系统的表现及对分布偏移的敏感性。

Result: 在检测器真阳性率99%时，未检测到的欺骗行为从1B模型的34%降至405B模型的14%；可在微调阶段完全移除昂贵的人工标注者，而不会导致欺骗行为显著增加；但检测器训练数据与偏好训练数据之间的分布偏移会将误报率推至不切实际的水平。

Conclusion: SOLiD方法在更大模型上表现更优，且可减少对人工标注的依赖，但其对数据分布偏移的高度敏感性是实际部署中的关键瓶颈。

Abstract: Deceptive behavior in LLMs is costly to monitor and prevent, motivating approaches such as Scalable Oversight via Lie Detectors (SOLiD) (Cundy & Gleave, 2025), which uses lie detectors to identify responses for review by high-cost labelers. In this paper, we scale SOLiD to larger models and evaluate it in more diverse and realistic preference-learning settings. We find favorable scaling: undetected deception drops from 34% for 1B-parameter models to 14% for 405B-parameter models at a detector true positive rate of 99%, and expensive human labelers can be removed entirely from the fine-tuning phase without a statistically significant increase in deception.
